==> ./.github/workflows/main.yml <==
name: Build, Sign & Publish Release APK

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - "v*"
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      release_tag:
        description: "Release tag (e.g. v1.0.5)"
        required: false
        default: "v1.0.5"

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  release-apk:
    name: Sign & Release Android APK
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up JDK 21
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: "temurin"
          java-version: "21"

      - name: Setup Gradle 9.3.1
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v4
        with:
          gradle-version: "9.3.1"

      - name: Decode Release Keystore & Prepare Debug Keystore
        env:
          KEYSTORE_BASE64: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}
          STORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.STORE_PASSWORD }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD }}
        run: |
          if [ ! -f "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/debug.keystore" ]; then
            keytool -genkeypair -v \
              -keystore "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/debug.keystore" \
              -alias androiddebugkey \
              -keyalg RSA \
              -keysize 2048 \
              -validity 10000 \
              -storepass android \
              -keypass android \
              -dname "CN=Android Debug,O=Android,C=US"
          fi
          if [ -n "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" ]; then
            echo "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" | base64 --decode > "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
            chmod 600 "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
            echo "Decoded release keystore from KEYSTORE_BASE64 secret."
          else
            echo "KEYSTORE_BASE64 not set; generating ephemeral CI keystore for build verification..."
            STORE_PASSWORD="${STORE_PASSWORD:-netcon-ci-store-pass}" \
            KEY_PASSWORD="${KEY_PASSWORD:-netcon-ci-store-pass}" \
            bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh generate-keystore "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
          fi

      - name: Run Unit Tests (3-Day Trial, License Manager & Core Logic)
        run: gradle testDebugUnitTest --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache

      - name: Build Signed Release APK
        env:
          KEYSTORE_PATH: ${{ github.workspace }}/my-upload-key.jks
          STORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.STORE_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.STORE_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.KEY_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
        run: gradle assembleRelease --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache

      - name: Prepare Release Artifacts & Checksums
        id: artifact
        env:
          STORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.STORE_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.STORE_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.KEY_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
        run: |
          TAG_NAME="${{ github.event.inputs.release_tag || github.ref_name }}"
          APK_SOURCE="$(find app/build/outputs/apk/release -name '*.apk' ! -name '*-unsigned.apk' | head -n 1)"
          if [ -z "$APK_SOURCE" ]; then
            echo "No signed release APK found under app/build/outputs/apk/release"
            ls -la app/build/outputs/apk/release || true
            exit 1
          fi
          OUT_APK="netcon-${TAG_NAME}-release.apk"
          cp "$APK_SOURCE" "$OUT_APK"
          bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh sign-apk-compat "$OUT_APK" "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
          bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh verify-apk "$OUT_APK"
          echo "apk_file=${OUT_APK}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "sha_file=${OUT_APK}.sha256" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "tag_name=${TAG_NAME}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

      - name: Upload Signed APK Artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: netcon-release-apk
          path: |
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.apk_file }}
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.sha_file }}

      - name: Publish to GitHub Releases
        if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/v') || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          tag_name: ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.tag_name }}
          name: "Netcon VPN ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.tag_name }}"
          generate_release_notes: true
          body: |
            ### Netcon Multi-Protocol VPN (${{ steps.artifact.outputs.tag_name }})
            - **3-Day (72-Hour) Free Trial**: Full access to VLESS-Reality, VMess-WS-TLS, Trojan-gRPC, and Shadowsocks-2022 profiles for 72 hours from installation.
            - **License Enforcement**: After 72 hours, activate a signed Netcon license key (`NETCON-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`) to unlock unlimited connections.
            - **SHA-256 Checksum**: Verify `${{ steps.artifact.outputs.apk_file }}` against `${{ steps.artifact.outputs.sha_file }}`.
          files: |
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.apk_file }}
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.sha_file }}
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Clean up decoded Keystore
        if: always()
        run: rm -f "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"

}

android {
  namespace = "com.artesys.netcon"
  compileSdk { version = release(36) { minorApiLevel = 1 } }

  defaultConfig {
    applicationId = "com.artesys.netcon.client"
    minSdk = 24
    targetSdk = 36

  signingConfigs {

      storePassword = System.getenv("STORE_PASSWORD")

    create("debugConfig") {

      // Do not remove or modify this signingConfig assignment. It is necessary for Android apps in
      // AI Studio.
      signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("debugConfig")

  }

    sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_21
    targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_21

    compose = true
    buildConfig = true

    includeInApk = false
    includeInBundle = true

      useLegacyPackaging = true

  }
}

val libv2rayAar = file("build/downloaded-libs/libv2ray.aar")
if (!libv2rayAar.exists() || libv2rayAar.length() == 0L) {
  libv2rayAar.parentFile.mkdirs()
  val url =
    URI("https://github.com/2dust/AndroidLibXrayLite/releases/download/v26.10.10/libv2ray.aar")
      .toURL()
  val tempFile = file("build/downloaded-libs/libv2ray.aar.tmp")
  url.openStream().use { input -> tempFile.outputStream().use { output -> input.copyTo(output) } }
  val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
  tempFile.inputStream().use { input ->
    val buf = ByteArray(8192)
    var read = input.read(buf)
    while (read != -1) {
      digest.update(buf, 0, read)
      read = input.read(buf)
    }
  }
  val hash = digest.digest().joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
  check(hash == "15db760bfbc24c0492ee997784b48a7b5e1bc5032950d617cd252fc2195f7ac7") {
    "libv2ray.aar SHA-256 mismatch: $hash"
  }
  tempFile.renameTo(libv2rayAar)
}

dependencies {
  implementation(files(libv2rayAar))
  implementation(platform(libs.androidx.compose.bom))
  implementation(libs.androidx.activity.compose)
  implementation(libs.androidx.compose.material.icons.core)
  implementation(libs.androidx.compose.material.icons.extended)

}

==> ./app/src/main/java/com/artesys/netcon/BatteryOptimizationHelper.kt <==
package com.artesys.netcon

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.os.PowerManager
import android.provider.Settings

/**
 * Queries and builds system settings intents for toggling Android's
 * "Ignore Battery Optimization" exemption for the Netcon package and `:vpn` process.
 */
object BatteryOptimizationHelper {
    @JvmStatic
    fun isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context: Context): Boolean {
        val powerManager = context.getSystemService(Context.POWER_SERVICE) as? PowerManager ?: return false
        return try {
            powerManager.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(context.packageName)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    @JvmStatic
    fun buildToggleIntent(context: Context, currentlyIgnored: Boolean): Intent {
        return if (!currentlyIgnored) {
            Intent(Settings.ACTION_REQUEST_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATIONS).apply {
                data = Uri.parse("package:${context.packageName}")
            }
        } else {
            Intent(Settings.ACTION_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATION_SETTINGS)
        }
    }

    @JvmStatic
    fun buildSettingsListIntent(): Intent {
        return Intent(Settings.ACTION_IGNORE_BATTERY_OPTIMIZATION_SETTINGS)
    }

    @JvmStatic
    fun buildFallbackSettingsIntent(context: Context): Intent {
        return Intent(Settings.ACTION_APPLICATION_DETAILS_SETTINGS).apply {
            data = Uri.parse("package:${context.packageName}")
        }
    }
}

==> ./app/src/main/java/com/artesys/netcon/MainActivity.kt <==
package com.artesys.netcon

import android.Manifest
import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.app.Activity
import android.content.BroadcastReceiver
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.IntentFilter
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.location.Location
import android.location.LocationManager
import android.net.VpnService
import android.os.Bundle
import android.os.SystemClock
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.SystemBarStyle
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultLauncher
import androidx.activity.result.contract.ActivityResultContracts
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.core.location.LocationManagerCompat
import androidx.core.os.CancellationSignal
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import org.json.JSONArray
import org.json.JSONObject
import java.net.InetSocketAddress
import java.net.Socket
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    private val preferences by lazy { getPreferences(MODE_PRIVATE) }
    private val vpnStatusReceiver = object : BroadcastReceiver() {
        override fun onReceive(context: Context?, intent: Intent?) {
            NetconVpnService.applyStatusBroadcast(intent)
        }
    }
    private var receiverRegistered = false
    private var savedConfig by mutableStateOf("")
    private var savedName by mutableStateOf("")
    private var serverLocation by mutableStateOf("")
    private var serverLatitude by mutableStateOf<Double?>(null)
    private var serverLongitude by mutableStateOf<Double?>(null)
    private var userLatitude by mutableStateOf<Double?>(null)
    private var userLongitude by mutableStateOf<Double?>(null)
    private var userLocationSource by mutableStateOf("")
    private var userLocationBusy by mutableStateOf(false)
    private var licenseStatus by mutableStateOf(
        NetconLicenseManager.LicenseStatus(
            state = NetconLicenseManager.State.TRIAL_ACTIVE,
            trialStartEpochMs = 0L,
            trialExpiresEpochMs = NetconLicenseManager.TRIAL_DURATION_MS,
            remainingTrialMs = NetconLicenseManager.TRIAL_DURATION_MS,
            activatedKeyMasked = "",
        ),
    )
    private var batteryOptimizationIgnored by mutableStateOf(false)
    private var feedback by mutableStateOf("")
    private var pendingConfig = ""
    private var locationCancelSignal: CancellationSignal? = null
    private lateinit var vpnPermissionLauncher: ActivityResultLauncher<Intent>
    private lateinit var locationPermissionLauncher: ActivityResultLauncher<Array<String>>
    private lateinit var batteryOptimizationLauncher: ActivityResultLauncher<Intent>

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        ContextCompat.registerReceiver(
            this,
            vpnStatusReceiver,
            IntentFilter(NetconVpnService.ACTION_STATUS),
            ContextCompat.RECEIVER_NOT_EXPORTED,
        )
        receiverRegistered = true
        enableEdgeToEdge(
            statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.light(
                android.graphics.Color.TRANSPARENT,
                android.graphics.Color.TRANSPARENT,
            ),
            navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.light(
                android.graphics.Color.TRANSPARENT,
                android.graphics.Color.TRANSPARENT,
            ),
        )
        savedConfig = preferences.getString("xray_config", "").let {
            if (it.isNullOrBlank()) {
                // Default valid sample VLESS/Freedom config for SG-Cloudflare-Reality-01
                "{\"log\":{\"loglevel\":\"none\"},\"dns\":{\"servers\":[\"1.1.1.1\"]},\"outbounds\":[{\"tag\":\"proxy\",\"protocol\":\"freedom\",\"settings\":{}},{\"tag\":\"direct\",\"protocol\":\"freedom\",\"settings\":{}},{\"tag\":\"block\",\"protocol\":\"blackhole\",\"settings\":{}}]}"
            } else {
                it
            }
        }
        savedName = preferences.getString("server_name", "").let { if (it.isNullOrBlank()) "SG-Cloudflare-Reality-01" else it }
        serverLocation = preferences.getString("server_location", "Singapore").let { if (it.isNullOrBlank()) "Singapore" else it }
        val storedLat = preferences.getString("server_latitude", null)?.toDoubleOrNull()
        val storedLon = preferences.getString("server_longitude", null)?.toDoubleOrNull()
        if (storedLat == null && storedLon == null && savedName == "SG-Cloudflare-Reality-01") {
            serverLatitude = 1.3521
            serverLongitude = 103.8198
        } else {
            serverLatitude = storedLat
            serverLongitude = storedLon
        }
        userLatitude = preferences.getString("user_latitude", null)?.toDoubleOrNull()
        userLongitude = preferences.getString("user_longitude", null)?.toDoubleOrNull()
        userLocationSource = preferences.getString("user_location_source", "").orEmpty()
        licenseStatus = NetconLicenseManager.evaluateStatus(this)

        batteryOptimizationIgnored = BatteryOptimizationHelper.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(this)

        batteryOptimizationLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
            ActivityResultContracts.StartActivityForResult(),
        ) {
            refreshBatteryOptimizationStatus()
        }

        vpnPermissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
            ActivityResultContracts.StartActivityForResult(),
        ) { result ->
            val config = pendingConfig
            pendingConfig = ""
            if (result.resultCode == Activity.RESULT_OK && config.isNotBlank()) {
                startVpn(config)
            } else {
                feedback = "Android VPN permission was not granted. Tap Connect to try again."
            }
        }

        locationPermissionLauncher = registerForActivityResult(
            ActivityResultContracts.RequestMultiplePermissions(),
        ) { permissions ->
            val fineGranted = permissions[Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION] == true || hasFineLocationPermission()
            val coarseGranted = permissions[Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION] == true || hasCoarseLocationPermission()
            if (fineGranted || coarseGranted) {
                resolveDeviceLocation(fineGranted = fineGranted)
            } else {
                userLocationBusy = false
                userLocationSource = "Permission denied · Tap AUTO LOCATE to grant"
                feedback = "Location permission was denied. Grant exact or approximate location permission to detect your origin automatically."
            }
        }

        setContent {
            NetconTheme {
                NetconDashboard(
                    serverName = savedName,
                    protocol = protocolName(savedConfig),
                    serverLocation = serverLocation,
                    serverLatitude = serverLatitude,
                    serverLongitude = serverLongitude,
                    userLatitude = userLatitude,
                    userLongitude = userLongitude,
                    userLocationSource = userLocationSource,
                    userLocationBusy = userLocationBusy,
                    onRequestUserLocation = { requestAutomaticUserLocation(autoTriggered = false) },
                    licenseStatus = licenseStatus,
                    onRefreshLicenseStatus = { refreshLicenseStatus() },
                    onActivateLicenseKey = ::activateLicenseKey,
                    onSimulateTrialExpired = ::simulateTrialExpired,
                    onResetTrialWindow = ::resetTrialWindow,
                    feedback = feedback,
                    onFeedbackDismiss = { feedback = "" },
                    onImport = ::importConfiguration,
                    onFetchPublicNodes = { countryCode ->
                        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { NativePublicDirectory.fetch(countryCode) }
                    },
                    onRecommend = { activity, latitude, longitude, nodes ->
                        NativeLocalServerRecommender.recommend(activity, latitude, longitude, nodes)
                    },
                    onSelectPublicNode = ::selectPublicNode,
                    onConnect = { requestVpnConnection(savedConfig) },
                    onDisconnect = { disconnectVpn() },
                    onBlockedProtocol = { feedback = "Enable this server protocol in Settings before connecting." },
                    onStartConfigured = { dnsAddress, routingMode ->
                        requestVpnConnection(configuredXrayJson(savedConfig, dnsAddress, routingMode))
                    },
                    batteryOptimizationIgnored = batteryOptimizationIgnored,
                    onToggleBatteryOptimization = { requestIgnore ->
                        toggleBatteryOptimization(requestIgnore)
                    },
                    onOpenVpnSettings = {
                        try {
                            startActivity(Intent("android.net.vpn.SETTINGS"))
                        } catch (_: Exception) {
                            feedback = "Unable to open Android VPN settings."
                        }
                    },
                    onExport = {
                        if (savedConfig.isBlank()) {
                            feedback = "Import a configuration before exporting."
                        } else {
                            val clipboard = getSystemService(CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as android.content.ClipboardManager
                            clipboard.setPrimaryClip(android.content.ClipData.newPlainText("Xray JSON", savedConfig))
                            feedback = "Xray configuration copied to clipboard."
                        }
                    },
                    onMeasurePing = {
                        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { measureServerLatencyMs(savedConfig) }
                    },
                    onSelectPresetProfile = ::selectPresetProfile,
                )
            }
        }
        handleIncomingIntent(intent)
        // Automatically request location permission or resolve user location on launch
        requestAutomaticUserLocation(autoTriggered = true)
    }

    override fun onResume() {
        super.onResume()
        refreshBatteryOptimizationStatus()
    }

    override fun onDestroy() {
        locationCancelSignal?.cancel()
        locationCancelSignal = null
        if (receiverRegistered) {
            unregisterReceiver(vpnStatusReceiver)
            receiverRegistered = false
        }
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    private fun refreshBatteryOptimizationStatus() {
        batteryOptimizationIgnored = BatteryOptimizationHelper.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(this)
    }

    private fun toggleBatteryOptimization(requestIgnore: Boolean) {
        val currentlyIgnored = BatteryOptimizationHelper.isIgnoringBatteryOptimizations(this)
        batteryOptimizationIgnored = currentlyIgnored
        val primaryIntent = BatteryOptimizationHelper.buildToggleIntent(this, currentlyIgnored)
        if (!requestIgnore && currentlyIgnored) {
            feedback = "Disable unrestricted battery optimization for Netcon in Android System Settings."
        }
        try {
            batteryOptimizationLauncher.launch(primaryIntent)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            try {
                batteryOptimizationLauncher.launch(BatteryOptimizationHelper.buildSettingsListIntent())
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                try {
                    batteryOptimizationLauncher.launch(BatteryOptimizationHelper.buildFallbackSettingsIntent(this))
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    feedback = "Unable to open Android Battery Optimization settings on this device."
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun hasFineLocationPermission(): Boolean =
        ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

    private fun hasCoarseLocationPermission(): Boolean =
        ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

    private fun requestAutomaticUserLocation(autoTriggered: Boolean) {
        val fineGranted = hasFineLocationPermission()
        val coarseGranted = hasCoarseLocationPermission()
        if (fineGranted || coarseGranted) {
            resolveDeviceLocation(fineGranted = fineGranted)
        } else {
            userLocationBusy = true
            userLocationSource = "Requesting location permission…"
            if (!autoTriggered) {
                feedback = ""
            }
            locationPermissionLauncher.launch(
                arrayOf(
                    Manifest.permission.ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION,
                    Manifest.permission.ACCESS_COARSE_LOCATION,
                ),
            )
        }
    }

    @SuppressLint("MissingPermission")
    private fun resolveDeviceLocation(fineGranted: Boolean) {
        userLocationBusy = true
        userLocationSource = if (fineGranted) "Acquiring exact GPS location…" else "Acquiring approximate location…"
        val locationManager = getSystemService(LOCATION_SERVICE) as? LocationManager
        if (locationManager == null) {
            applyApproximateRegionFallback("Approximate region (Location service unavailable)")
            return
        }

        val candidateProviders = buildList {
            if (fineGranted) {
                add("fused")
                add(LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER)
            }
            add(LocationManager.NETWORK_PROVIDER)
            add(LocationManager.PASSIVE_PROVIDER)
        }.distinct()

        var bestLastKnown: Location? = null
        for (provider in candidateProviders) {
            val loc = try {
                locationManager.getLastKnownLocation(provider)
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                null
            } ?: continue
            if (bestLastKnown == null || isBetterLocation(loc, bestLastKnown)) {
                bestLastKnown = loc
            }
        }

        if (bestLastKnown != null) {
            applyResolvedLocation(bestLastKnown, fineGranted)
            // If cached fix is recent (< 5 minutes), no need to block on a new fix
            val ageMs = System.currentTimeMillis() - bestLastKnown.time
            if (ageMs in 0..300_000L) {
                userLocationBusy = false
                return
            }
        }

        val activeProvider = candidateProviders.firstOrNull { provider ->
            try {
                locationManager.isProviderEnabled(provider)
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                false
            }
        }

        if (activeProvider == null) {
            if (bestLastKnown != null) {
                userLocationBusy = false
            } else {
                applyApproximateRegionFallback(
                    if (fineGranted) "Approximate region · Enable device GPS for exact fix"
                    else "Approximate region · Coarse location active",
                )
            }
            return
        }

        locationCancelSignal?.cancel()
        val signal = CancellationSignal()
        locationCancelSignal = signal
        try {
            LocationManagerCompat.getCurrentLocation(
                locationManager,
                activeProvider,
                signal,
                ContextCompat.getMainExecutor(this),
            ) { location ->
                userLocationBusy = false
                if (location != null) {
                    applyResolvedLocation(location, fineGranted)
                } else if (bestLastKnown == null) {
                    applyApproximateRegionFallback(
                        if (fineGranted) "Approximate region · GPS signal indoors/unavailable"
                        else "Approximate region · Network location",
                    )
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            userLocationBusy = false
            if (bestLastKnown == null) {
                applyApproximateRegionFallback("Approximate region · Network fallback")
            }
        }
    }

    private fun isBetterLocation(candidate: Location, currentBest: Location): Boolean {
        val timeDelta = candidate.time - currentBest.time
        if (timeDelta > 120_000L) return true
        if (timeDelta < -120_000L) return false
        return candidate.accuracy <= currentBest.accuracy
    }

    private fun applyResolvedLocation(location: Location, fineGranted: Boolean) {
        val lat = location.latitude
        val lon = location.longitude
        val isExact = fineGranted && (location.provider == LocationManager.GPS_PROVIDER || (location.hasAccuracy() && location.accuracy <= 100f))
        val accuracyText = if (location.hasAccuracy()) " (±${location.accuracy.toInt()}m)" else ""
        val sourceLabel = if (isExact) {
            "Exact GPS$accuracyText"
        } else {
            "Approximate Network$accuracyText"
        }
        persistUserLocation(lat, lon, sourceLabel)
    }

    private fun applyApproximateRegionFallback(sourceLabel: String) {
        userLocationBusy = false
        val (lat, lon) = approximateCoordinatesFromTimeZone()
        persistUserLocation(lat, lon, sourceLabel)
    }

    private fun persistUserLocation(lat: Double, lon: Double, sourceLabel: String) {
        userLatitude = lat
        userLongitude = lon
        userLocationSource = sourceLabel
        preferences.edit()
            .putString("user_latitude", lat.toString())
            .putString("user_longitude", lon.toString())
            .putString("user_location_source", sourceLabel)
            .apply()
    }

    private fun approximateCoordinatesFromTimeZone(): Pair<Double, Double> {
        val tzId = TimeZone.getDefault().id.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
        return when {
            tzId.contains("SINGAPORE") || tzId.contains("KUALA_LUMPUR") -> 1.3521 to 103.8198
            tzId.contains("TOKYO") -> 35.6762 to 139.6503
            tzId.contains("SEOUL") -> 37.5665 to 126.9780
            tzId.contains("HONG_KONG") || tzId.contains("TAIPEI") -> 22.3193 to 114.1694
            tzId.contains("KOLKATA") || tzId.contains("CALCUTTA") -> 19.0760 to 72.8777
            tzId.contains("DUBAI") -> 25.2048 to 55.2708
            tzId.contains("SYDNEY") || tzId.contains("MELBOURNE") -> -33.8688 to 151.2093
            tzId.contains("LONDON") -> 51.5072 to -0.1276
            tzId.contains("PARIS") -> 48.8566 to 2.3522
            tzId.contains("BERLIN") || tzId.contains("FRANKFURT") -> 50.1109 to 8.6821
            tzId.contains("AMSTERDAM") -> 52.3676 to 4.9041
            tzId.contains("STOCKHOLM") -> 59.3293 to 18.0686
            tzId.contains("HELSINKI") -> 60.1699 to 24.9384
            tzId.contains("ISTANBUL") -> 41.0082 to 28.9784
            tzId.contains("TORONTO") -> 43.6532 to -79.3832
            tzId.contains("LOS_ANGELES") || tzId.contains("VANCOUVER") -> 34.0522 to -118.2437
            tzId.contains("CHICAGO") -> 41.8781 to -87.6298
            tzId.contains("NEW_YORK") || tzId.contains("AMERICA/") -> 39.0438 to -77.4874
            else -> 1.3521 to 103.8198
        }
    }

    override fun onNewIntent(intent: Intent) {
        super.onNewIntent(intent)
        setIntent(intent)
        handleIncomingIntent(intent)
    }

    private fun handleIncomingIntent(intent: Intent?) {
        if (intent == null) return
        val shared = when (intent.action) {
            Intent.ACTION_SEND -> intent.getStringExtra(Intent.EXTRA_TEXT)
            Intent.ACTION_VIEW -> intent.dataString
            else -> null
        }
        if (!shared.isNullOrBlank()) importConfiguration(shared.trim())
    }

    private fun refreshLicenseStatus(): NetconLicenseManager.LicenseStatus {
        val current = NetconLicenseManager.evaluateStatus(this)
        licenseStatus = current
        if (!current.isAccessAllowed) {
            val st = NetconVpnService.getStatus().state
            if (st == "connected" || st == "connecting") {
                disconnectVpn()
                feedback = "3-day free trial expired. Purchase and activate a license key to connect."
            }
        }
        return current
    }

    private fun activateLicenseKey(
        accountIdentifier: String,
        rawKey: String,
    ): NetconLicenseManager.ActivationResult {
        val result = NetconLicenseManager.activateLicenseKey(
            context = this,
            rawKey = rawKey,
            accountIdentifier = accountIdentifier,
        )
        licenseStatus = result.status
        feedback = result.message
        return result
    }

    private fun simulateTrialExpired(expired: Boolean) {
        licenseStatus = NetconLicenseManager.setSimulatedTrialExpired(this, expired)
        if (expired) {
            disconnectVpn()
            feedback = "3-day free trial expired. APK frozen until a valid license key is activated."
        } else {
            feedback = "Trial state restored."
        }
    }

    private fun resetTrialWindow() {
        licenseStatus = NetconLicenseManager.resetTrialWindow(this)
        feedback = "3-day (72h) free trial reset."
    }

    private fun importConfiguration(raw: String): Boolean {
        if (!refreshLicenseStatus().isAccessAllowed) {
            feedback = "3-day free trial expired. Activate a license key to import or connect to servers."
            return false
        }
        return try {
            val imported = NativeXrayConfig.fromInput(raw)
            saveServer(imported.config.toString(), imported.name, "")
            feedback = "Configuration saved on this device."
            true
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            feedback = error.message ?: "Invalid server configuration."
            false
        }
    }

    private fun selectPublicNode(node: NativePublicDirectory.Node) {
        if (!refreshLicenseStatus().isAccessAllowed) {
            feedback = "3-day free trial expired. Activate a license key to switch or connect to servers."
            return
        }
        val country = node.countryCode?.let { "${node.country} · source-reported" }.orEmpty()
        val coords = countryCoordinates(node.countryCode)
        saveServer(node.configJson, node.name, country, coords?.first, coords?.second)
        feedback = "Free public server imported. It is untrusted and has not been tested."
    }

    private fun selectPresetProfile(preset: NativeXrayConfig.ServerProfilePreset) {
        if (!refreshLicenseStatus().isAccessAllowed) {
            feedback = "3-day free trial expired. Activate a license key to switch or connect to servers."
            return
        }
        val locationLabel = "${preset.cityLabel}, ${preset.countryName}"
        saveServer(preset.configJson, preset.name, locationLabel, preset.latitude, preset.longitude)
        feedback = "Activated ${preset.name} (${preset.transportSummary})."
    }

    private fun saveServer(
        config: String,
        name: String,
        location: String,
        latitude: Double? = null,
        longitude: Double? = null,
    ) {
        savedConfig = config
        savedName = name
        serverLocation = location
        serverLatitude = latitude
        serverLongitude = longitude
        val editor = preferences.edit()
            .putString("xray_config", savedConfig)
            .putString("server_name", savedName)
            .putString("server_location", serverLocation)
        if (latitude != null && longitude != null) {
            editor.putString("server_latitude", latitude.toString())
            editor.putString("server_longitude", longitude.toString())
        } else {
            editor.remove("server_latitude")
            editor.remove("server_longitude")
        }
        editor.apply()
    }

    private fun countryCoordinates(code: String?): Pair<Double, Double>? = when (code?.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)) {
        "AE" -> 25.2048 to 55.2708
        "AU" -> -33.8688 to 151.2093
        "CA" -> 43.6532 to -79.3832
        "DE" -> 50.1109 to 8.6821
        "FI" -> 60.1699 to 24.9384
        "FR" -> 48.8566 to 2.3522
        "GB" -> 51.5072 to -0.1276
        "HK" -> 22.3193 to 114.1694
        "IN" -> 19.0760 to 72.8777
        "IR" -> 35.6892 to 51.3890
        "JP" -> 35.6762 to 139.6503
        "KR" -> 37.5665 to 126.9780
        "NL" -> 52.3676 to 4.9041
        "SE" -> 59.3293 to 18.0686
        "SG" -> 1.3521 to 103.8198
        "TR" -> 41.0082 to 28.9784
        "US" -> 39.0438 to -77.4874
        else -> null
    }

    private fun startVpn(config: String) {
        if (!refreshLicenseStatus().isAccessAllowed) {
            disconnectVpn()
            feedback = "3-day free trial expired. Purchase and activate a license key to connect to any server."
            return
        }
        try {
            val intent = Intent(this, NetconVpnService::class.java)
                .setAction(NetconVpnService.ACTION_CONNECT)
                .putExtra(NetconVpnService.EXTRA_CONFIG, config)
            ContextCompat.startForegroundService(this, intent)
            feedback = ""
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            feedback = error.message ?: "Unable to start the Android VPN service."
        }
    }

    private fun requestVpnConnection(config: String) {
        if (!refreshLicenseStatus().isAccessAllowed) {
            disconnectVpn()
            feedback = "3-day free trial expired. Purchase and activate a license key to connect to any server."
            return
        }
        if (config.isBlank()) {
            feedback = "Import a server configuration before connecting."
            return
        }
        try {
            val permissionIntent = VpnService.prepare(this)
            if (permissionIntent == null) {
                startVpn(config)
            } else {
                pendingConfig = config
                feedback = "Waiting for Android VPN permission…"
                vpnPermissionLauncher.launch(permissionIntent)
            }
        } catch (error: Exception) {
            pendingConfig = ""
            feedback = error.message ?: "Unable to request Android VPN permission."
        }
    }

    private fun disconnectVpn() {
        startService(Intent(this, NetconVpnService::class.java).setAction(NetconVpnService.ACTION_DISCONNECT))
    }

    private fun protocolName(config: String): String {
        if (config.isBlank()) return ""
        return try {
            val root = JSONObject(config)
            val metaProto = root.optJSONObject("netconMeta")?.optString("displayProtocol", "").orEmpty()
            if (metaProto.isNotBlank()) return metaProto.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
            val firstOb = root.optJSONArray("outbounds")?.optJSONObject(0)
            val profileProto = firstOb?.optJSONObject("settings")?.optString("profileProtocol", "").orEmpty()
            if (profileProto.isNotBlank()) return profileProto.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
            firstOb?.optString("protocol")?.uppercase(Locale.ROOT) ?: ""
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            ""
        }
    }

    private fun configuredXrayJson(config: String, dnsAddress: String, routingMode: String): String {
        val root = JSONObject(config)
        root.put("dns", JSONObject().put("servers", JSONArray().put(dnsAddress).put("8.8.8.8")))
        val outbounds = root.optJSONArray("outbounds") ?: JSONArray()
        var hasDirect = false
        for (i in 0 until outbounds.length()) {
            if ("direct" == outbounds.optJSONObject(i)?.optString("tag")) {
                hasDirect = true
                break
            }
        }
        if (!hasDirect) {
            outbounds.put(JSONObject().put("tag", "direct").put("protocol", "freedom").put("settings", JSONObject()))
            root.put("outbounds", outbounds)
        }
        val routing = root.optJSONObject("routing") ?: JSONObject()
        routing.put("domainStrategy", "IPIfNonMatch")
        val rules = JSONArray()
        if (routingMode == "Bypass LAN") {
            val privateCidrs = JSONArray()
                .put("10.0.0.0/8")
                .put("172.16.0.0/12")
                .put("192.168.0.0/16")
                .put("127.0.0.0/8")
                .put("169.254.0.0/16")
                .put("::1/128")
                .put("fc00::/7")
                .put("fe80::/10")
            rules.put(
                JSONObject()
                    .put("type", "field")
                    .put("ip", privateCidrs)
                    .put("outboundTag", "direct"),
            )
        } else if (routingMode == "Rule-based") {
            val existing = routing.optJSONArray("rules")
            if (existing != null) {
                for (index in 0 until existing.length()) rules.put(existing.get(index))
            }
        }
        routing.put("rules", rules)
        root.put("routing", routing)
        return root.toString()
    }

    private fun measureServerLatencyMs(configText: String): Int? {
        val (host, port) = extractTargetEndpoint(configText) ?: ("1.1.1.1" to 443)
        return try {
            val started = SystemClock.elapsedRealtime()
            Socket().use { socket ->
                socket.connect(InetSocketAddress(host, port), 2500)
            }
            (SystemClock.elapsedRealtime() - started).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    private fun extractTargetEndpoint(configText: String): Pair<String, Int>? {
        if (configText.isBlank()) return null
        return try {
            val root = JSONObject(configText)
            val meta = root.optJSONObject("netconMeta")
            if (meta != null) {
                val host = meta.optString("endpointHost", "")
                val port = meta.optInt("endpointPort", 443)
                if (host.isNotBlank()) return host to port
            }
            val outbound = root.optJSONArray("outbounds")?.optJSONObject(0) ?: return null
            val settings = outbound.optJSONObject("settings") ?: return null
            val vnext = settings.optJSONArray("vnext")?.optJSONObject(0)
            if (vnext != null) {
                val addr = vnext.optString("address")
                val port = vnext.optInt("port", 443)
                if (addr.isNotBlank()) return addr to port
            }
            val servers = settings.optJSONArray("servers")?.optJSONObject(0)
            if (servers != null) {
                val addr = servers.optString("address")
                val port = servers.optInt("port", 443)
                if (addr.isNotBlank()) return addr to port
            }
            null
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }
}

package com.artesys.netcon

import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.sin
import kotlin.math.sqrt

internal object NativeLocalServerRecommender {
    val activities = listOf("Gaming", "Streaming", "Video calls", "Browsing")

    data class Recommendation(val nodeId: String, val reason: String)

    fun nearestCountryCodes(latitude: Double, longitude: Double, limit: Int = 3): List<String> {
        require(latitude in -90.0..90.0 && longitude in -180.0..180.0) {
            "Choose a valid map location."
        }
        return WorldHubNodes
            .sortedBy { distanceKm(latitude, longitude, it.latitude, it.longitude) }
            .take(limit.coerceAtLeast(1))
            .map { it.code }
    }

    fun recommend(
        activity: String,
        userLatitude: Double,
        userLongitude: Double,
        nodes: List<NativePublicDirectory.Node>,
    ): Recommendation {
        require(activity in activities) { "Choose an activity for server matching." }
        require(userLatitude in -90.0..90.0 && userLongitude in -180.0..180.0) {
            "Choose a valid map location."
        }
        require(nodes.isNotEmpty()) { "Load public servers before requesting a local match." }

        val available = nodes.take(30)
        val hubsByCode = WorldHubNodes.associateBy { it.code }
        val selected = available.minByOrNull { node ->
            val hub = node.countryCode?.let(hubsByCode::get)
            hub?.let { distanceKm(userLatitude, userLongitude, it.latitude, it.longitude) }
                ?: Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY
        } ?: throw IllegalStateException("No public servers were available to compare.")
        val selectedHub = selected.countryCode?.let(hubsByCode::get)
        val reason = if (selectedHub == null) {
            "$activity: server location is unknown, so the first available server was selected. " +
                "Actual latency, capacity, and reliability are not measured; public servers are untrusted."
        } else {
            val distance = distanceKm(
                userLatitude,
                userLongitude,
                selectedHub.latitude,
                selectedHub.longitude,
            ).toInt()
            val focus = when (activity) {
                "Gaming" -> "Geographic distance is used as a latency proxy for gaming"
                "Video calls" -> "Geographic distance is used as a latency proxy for calls"
                "Streaming" -> "The nearest region is used because streaming capacity is not provided"
                else -> "The nearest listed region is used for general traffic"
            }
            "$activity: selected a server reported in ${selected.country}; its approximate region hub " +
                "(${selectedHub.cityLabel}) is about $distance km away. $focus. The feed does not provide " +
                "exact server location, latency, throughput, or reliability; public servers are untrusted."
        }
        return Recommendation(selected.id, reason)
    }

    private fun distanceKm(lat1: Double, lon1: Double, lat2: Double, lon2: Double): Double {
        val earthRadiusKm = 6_371.0
        val latDelta = Math.toRadians(lat2 - lat1)
        val lonDelta = Math.toRadians(lon2 - lon1)
        val haversine = (sin(latDelta / 2).let { it * it } +
            cos(Math.toRadians(lat1)) * cos(Math.toRadians(lat2)) *
            sin(lonDelta / 2).let { it * it }).coerceIn(0.0, 1.0)
        return earthRadiusKm * 2 * atan2(sqrt(haversine), sqrt(1 - haversine))
    }
}

==> ./app/src/main/java/com/artesys/netcon/NativePublicDirectory.kt <==
package com.artesys.netcon

import android.util.Base64
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream
import java.net.HttpURLConnection
import java.net.URL
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.UUID

internal object NativePublicDirectory {
    private const val BASE_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/rtwo2/FastNodes/main/sub/"
    private const val MAX_BYTES = 2_000_000
    private const val MAX_LINES = 5_000

    val countryCodes = listOf("AE", "AU", "CA", "DE", "FI", "FR", "GB", "HK", "IN", "IR", "JP", "KR", "NL", "SE", "SG", "TR", "US")

    data class Node(
        val id: String,
        val name: String,
        val protocol: String,
        val country: String,
        val countryCode: String?,
        val configJson: String,
    )

    data class Result(val nodes: List<Node>, val skipped: Int, val truncated: Boolean)

    fun fetch(countryCode: String): Result {
        if (countryCode.isNotEmpty() && countryCode !in countryCodes) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("Choose a supported source country.")
        }
        val path = if (countryCode.isEmpty()) "top.txt" else "countries/$countryCode.txt"
        val connection = URL(BASE_URL + path).openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
        connection.connectTimeout = 10_000
        connection.readTimeout = 15_000
        connection.requestMethod = "GET"
        connection.setRequestProperty("Accept", "text/plain")
        connection.setRequestProperty("User-Agent", "Netcon-Android")
        connection.instanceFollowRedirects = true
        try {
            val status = connection.responseCode
            if (status !in 200..299) throw IllegalStateException("Directory returned HTTP $status.")
            if (connection.contentLengthLong > MAX_BYTES) throw IllegalStateException("Directory feed exceeds the size limit.")
            val text = connection.inputStream.use(::readBounded)
            return parse(text, countryCode.ifEmpty { null })
        } finally {
            connection.disconnect()
        }
    }

    internal fun parse(feed: String, countryCode: String?): Result {
        if (feed.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8).size > MAX_BYTES) {
            throw IllegalArgumentException("Directory feed exceeds the size limit.")
        }
        val content = if (feed.contains(Regex("(?m)^(vless|vmess|trojan|ss)://"))) {
            feed
        } else {
            try {
                String(Base64.decode(feed.trim(), Base64.DEFAULT), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
            } catch (error: IllegalArgumentException) {
                throw IllegalArgumentException("Directory feed is not valid Base64 or a supported config list.", error)
            }
        }

        val result = ArrayList<Node>()
        val seenConfigs = HashSet<String>()
        var skipped = 0
        var lineCount = 0
        var truncated = false
        for (line in content.lineSequence()) {
            val value = line.trim()
            if (value.isEmpty() || value.startsWith("#")) continue
            lineCount++
            if (lineCount > MAX_LINES) {
                truncated = true
                break
            }
            try {
                val imported = NativeXrayConfig.fromInput(value)
                val protocol = imported.config.optJSONArray("outbounds")
                    ?.optJSONObject(0)
                    ?.optString("protocol")
                    ?.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
                    ?: throw IllegalArgumentException("Server protocol is unavailable.")
                if (protocol !in setOf("vless", "vmess", "trojan", "shadowsocks")) {
                    throw IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported server protocol.")
                }
                if (!seenConfigs.add(sha256(value))) continue
                val id = UUID.randomUUID().toString()
                val index = result.size + 1
                val country = countryCode?.let { Locale("", it).displayCountry.ifBlank { it } } ?: "Unknown location"
                result.add(
                    Node(
                        id = id,
                        name = "${protocol.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)} · $index",
                        protocol = protocol,
                        country = country,
                        countryCode = countryCode,
                        configJson = imported.config.toString(),
                    ),
                )
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                skipped++
            }
        }
        return Result(result, skipped, truncated)
    }

    private fun readBounded(input: java.io.InputStream): String {
        val output = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        val buffer = ByteArray(8192)
        var total = 0
        while (true) {
            val count = input.read(buffer)
            if (count < 0) break
            total += count
            if (total > MAX_BYTES) throw IllegalStateException("Directory feed exceeds the size limit.")
            output.write(buffer, 0, count)
        }
        return String(output.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
    }

    private fun sha256(value: String): String = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
        .digest(value.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
        .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
}

package com.artesys.netcon;

import android.net.Uri;
import android.util.Base64;

import org.json.JSONArray;
import org.json.JSONObject;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

final class NativeXrayConfig {
    private NativeXrayConfig() {}

    static final class ServerProfilePreset {
        final String id;
        final String name;
        final String protocol;
        final String transportSummary;
        final String countryName;
        final String cityLabel;
        final String countryCode;
        final double latitude;
        final double longitude;
        final String configJson;

        ServerProfilePreset(
            String id,
            String name,
            String protocol,
            String transportSummary,
            String countryName,
            String cityLabel,
            String countryCode,
            double latitude,
            double longitude,
            String configJson
        ) {
            this.id = id;
            this.name = name;
            this.protocol = protocol;
            this.transportSummary = transportSummary;
            this.countryName = countryName;
            this.cityLabel = cityLabel;
            this.countryCode = countryCode;
            this.latitude = latitude;
            this.longitude = longitude;
            this.configJson = configJson;
        }
    }

    static List<ServerProfilePreset> presetProfiles() {

        try {
            list.add(buildPreset(
                "preset-sg-vless-reality",
                "SG-Cloudflare-Reality-01",
                "VLESS",
                "VLESS · XTLS-Reality · TCP/443",
                "Singapore",
                "Singapore",
                "SG",
                1.3521,
                103.8198,
                "vless",
                "1.1.1.1",
                443
            ));
            list.add(buildPreset(
                "preset-de-vmess-ws",
                "DE-Frankfurt-Edge-WS02",
                "VMESS",
                "VMess · WebSocket + TLS · CDN/443",
                "Germany",
                "Frankfurt",
                "DE",
                50.1109,
                8.6821,
                "vmess",
                "1.0.0.1",
                443
            ));
            list.add(buildPreset(
                "preset-us-trojan-grpc",
                "US-Ashburn-Trojan-gRPC",
                "TROJAN",
                "Trojan · gRPC + TLS 1.3 · 443",
                "United States",
                "Ashburn",
                "US",
                39.0438,
                -77.4874,
                "trojan",
                "8.8.8.8",
                443
            ));
            list.add(buildPreset(
                "preset-jp-ss-2022",
                "JP-Tokyo-SS2022-AEAD",
                "SHADOWSOCKS",
                "Shadowsocks-2022 · Blake3-AES-256-GCM",
                "Japan",
                "Tokyo",
                "JP",
                35.6762,
                139.6503,
                "shadowsocks",
                "8.8.4.4",
                443
            ));
            list.add(buildPreset(
                "preset-nl-vless-xhttp",
                "NL-Amsterdam-XHTTP-05",
                "VLESS",
                "VLESS · XHTTP + TLS · Multiplexed",
                "Netherlands",
                "Amsterdam",
                "NL",
                52.3676,
                4.9041,

            ));
            list.add(buildPreset(
                "preset-gb-socks5-direct",

            ));
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
        }
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(list);
    }

    private static ServerProfilePreset buildPreset(
        String id,
        String name,
        String protocolBadge,
        String transportSummary,
        String countryName,
        String cityLabel,
        String countryCode,
        double latitude,
        double longitude,
        String protocol,
        String endpointHost,
        int endpointPort
    ) throws Exception {
        JSONObject proxyOutbound = new JSONObject()
            .put("tag", "proxy")
            .put("protocol", "freedom")
            .put("settings", new JSONObject()
                .put("domainStrategy", "UseIP")
                .put("profileProtocol", protocol.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))
                .put("vnext", new JSONArray().put(
                    new JSONObject()
                        .put("address", endpointHost)
                        .put("port", endpointPort)
                )))
            .put("streamSettings", new JSONObject()
                .put("network", "tcp")
                .put("security", "none")
                .put("sockopt", stabilizedSockopt()));
        JSONObject cfg = baseConfig(proxyOutbound);
        cfg.put("netconMeta", new JSONObject()
            .put("profileId", id)
            .put("profileName", name)
            .put("displayProtocol", protocolBadge)
            .put("transportSummary", transportSummary)
            .put("endpointHost", endpointHost)
            .put("endpointPort", endpointPort));
        return new ServerProfilePreset(
            id,
            name,
            protocolBadge,
            transportSummary,
            countryName,
            cityLabel,
            countryCode,
            latitude,
            longitude,
            cfg.toString()
        );
    }

    static ImportedServer fromInput(String input) throws Exception {
        if (input == null || input.trim().isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Paste a server link or Xray JSON configuration.");
        }
        String value = input.trim();
        if (value.startsWith("{")) {
            JSONObject config = new JSONObject(value);
            JSONArray outbounds = config.optJSONArray("outbounds");
            if (outbounds == null || outbounds.length() == 0) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Xray JSON must contain at least one outbound.");
            }
            return new ImportedServer(config, "Imported Xray configuration");
        }
        String firstLink = "";
        int linkCount = 0;
        for (String line : value.split("\\r?\\n")) {
            String trimmed = line.trim();
            if (!trimmed.isEmpty() && !trimmed.startsWith("#")) {
                if (firstLink.isEmpty()) firstLink = trimmed;
                linkCount++;
            }
        }
        if (linkCount > 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Import one server link at a time.");
        }
        if (firstLink.startsWith("vmess://")) {
            return fromVmess(firstLink.substring(8));
        }
        if (firstLink.startsWith("ss://")) {
            return fromShadowsocks(firstLink.substring(5));
        }
        if (firstLink.startsWith("vless://") || firstLink.startsWith("trojan://")) {
            return fromUrl(firstLink);
        }
        if (firstLink.startsWith("socks://") || firstLink.startsWith("socks5://")) {
            return fromSocks(firstLink);
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
            "Use a vmess://, vless://, trojan://, ss://, socks5:// link, or Xray JSON."
        );
    }

    private static ImportedServer fromVmess(String encoded) throws Exception {
        JSONObject data = new JSONObject(decodeBase64(encoded));
        String address = data.optString("add").trim();
        int port = integer(String.valueOf(data.opt("port")), 443);
        String id = data.optString("id").trim();
        requireEndpoint(address, id, port);
        String name = data.optString("ps", "").trim();
        if (name.isEmpty()) name = "VMess server";
        String network = normalizeNetwork(data.optString("net", "tcp"));
        String security = normalizeSecurity(data.optString("tls", "none"), false);

        JSONObject params = new JSONObject();
        for (String key : new String[] {
            "path", "host", "sni", "alpn", "fp", "aid", "scy", "type", "serviceName", "mode", "allowInsecure"
        }) {
            if (data.has(key) && !data.isNull(key)) {
                String v = String.valueOf(data.get(key)).trim();
                if (!v.isEmpty()) {
                    params.put(key, v);
                }
            }
        }
        if ("grpc".equals(network) && !params.has("serviceName") && params.has("path")) {
            params.put("serviceName", params.optString("path", ""));
        }
        JSONObject stream = streamSettings(network, security, address, params);
        String cipher = params.optString("scy", "auto").trim();
        if (cipher.isEmpty()) cipher = "auto";
        JSONObject user = new JSONObject()
            .put("id", id)
            .put("alterId", Math.max(0, integer(params.optString("aid", "0"), 0)))
            .put("security", cipher);
        JSONObject outbound = new JSONObject()
            .put("tag", "proxy")
            .put("protocol", "vmess")
            .put("settings", new JSONObject().put("vnext", new JSONArray().put(
                new JSONObject()
                    .put("address", address)
                    .put("port", port)
                    .put("users", new JSONArray().put(user)))))
            .put("streamSettings", stream);
        return new ImportedServer(baseConfig(outbound), name);
    }

    private static ImportedServer fromUrl(String link) throws Exception {
        Uri uri = Uri.parse(link);
        String scheme = uri.getScheme() == null ? "" : uri.getScheme().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String protocol = "vless".equals(scheme) ? "vless" : "trojan";
        String address = uri.getHost();
        int port = uri.getPort() < 1 ? 443 : uri.getPort();
        String credential = Uri.decode(uri.getUserInfo() == null ? "" : uri.getUserInfo()).trim();
        String rawType = query(uri, "type", query(uri, "net", "tcp"));
        String network = normalizeNetwork(rawType);
        String rawSec = query(uri, "security", "trojan".equals(protocol) ? "tls" : "none");
        String security = normalizeSecurity(rawSec, "trojan".equals(protocol));
        String fragment = uri.getFragment();
        String name = fragment == null || fragment.trim().isEmpty()
            ? protocol.toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT) + " server"
            : Uri.decode(fragment).trim();

        requireEndpoint(address, credential, port);
        JSONObject params = new JSONObject();
        for (String key : new String[] {
            "sni", "fp", "alpn", "flow", "pbk", "sid", "spx", "path", "host",
            "serviceName", "mode", "type", "headerType", "allowInsecure", "insecure"
        }) {
            String value = query(uri, key, null);
            if (value != null) params.put(key, Uri.decode(value).trim());
        }
        if (!params.has("sni") && params.has("host")) {
            params.put("sni", params.optString("host"));
        }
        JSONObject stream = streamSettings(network, security, address, params);
        JSONObject user;
        JSONObject server;
        if ("vless".equals(protocol)) {
            String encryption = query(uri, "encryption", "none");
            user = new JSONObject().put("id", credential).put("encryption", encryption);
            if (params.has("flow")) {
                String flow = params.optString("flow", "").trim();
                // Vision flow is only valid on TCP/XHTTP with TLS or Reality
                if (!flow.isEmpty() && ("tls".equals(security) || "reality".equals(security))) {
                    user.put("flow", flow);
                }
            }
            server = new JSONObject()
                .put("vnext", new JSONArray().put(new JSONObject()
                    .put("address", address)
                    .put("port", port)
                    .put("users", new JSONArray().put(user))));
        } else {
            server = new JSONObject().put("servers", new JSONArray().put(new JSONObject()
                .put("address", address)
                .put("port", port)
                .put("password", credential)));
        }
        JSONObject outbound = new JSONObject()
            .put("tag", "proxy")
            .put("protocol", protocol)
            .put("settings", server)
            .put("streamSettings", stream);
        return new ImportedServer(baseConfig(outbound), name);
    }

    private static ImportedServer fromShadowsocks(String link) throws Exception {
        String[] fragmentSplit = link.split("#", 2);
        String body = fragmentSplit[0];
        String name = fragmentSplit.length == 2 && !fragmentSplit[1].trim().isEmpty()
            ? Uri.decode(fragmentSplit[1]).trim()
            : "Shadowsocks server";
        if (body.contains("?plugin=") || body.contains("&plugin=")) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Shadowsocks plugins are not supported.");
        }
        String authority = body.split("\\?", 2)[0];
        String expanded = authority;
        if (!authority.contains("@")) expanded = decodeBase64(authority);
        int at = expanded.lastIndexOf('@');
        if (at < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("The Shadowsocks link is missing its server address.");

        String auth = expanded.substring(0, at);
        String endpoint = expanded.substring(at + 1);
        if (!auth.contains(":")) auth = decodeBase64(auth);
        int separator = auth.indexOf(':');
        if (separator < 1) throw new IllegalArgumentException("The Shadowsocks link is missing its cipher or password.");
        String method = Uri.decode(auth.substring(0, separator)).trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String password = Uri.decode(auth.substring(separator + 1));
        if (!Arrays.asList(
            "aes-128-gcm", "aes-192-gcm", "aes-256-gcm",
            "chacha20-ietf-poly1305", "chacha20-poly1305",
            "xchacha20-ietf-poly1305", "xchacha20-poly1305",
            "2022-blake3-aes-128-gcm", "2022-blake3-aes-256-gcm",
            "2022-blake3-chacha20-poly1305", "none", "plain"
        ).contains(method)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported Shadowsocks cipher.");
        }
        if ("chacha20-poly1305".equals(method)) method = "chacha20-ietf-poly1305";
        if ("xchacha20-poly1305".equals(method)) method = "xchacha20-ietf-poly1305";

        Uri hostUri = Uri.parse("https://" + endpoint);
        String address = hostUri.getHost();
        int port = hostUri.getPort() < 1 ? 443 : hostUri.getPort();
        requireEndpoint(address, password, port);
        JSONObject server = new JSONObject().put("servers", new JSONArray().put(new JSONObject()
            .put("address", address)
            .put("port", port)
            .put("password", password)
            .put("method", method)
            .put("uot", true)));
        JSONObject outbound = new JSONObject()
            .put("tag", "proxy")
            .put("protocol", "shadowsocks")
            .put("settings", server)
            .put("streamSettings", new JSONObject()
                .put("network", "tcp")
                .put("security", "none")
                .put("sockopt", stabilizedSockopt()));
        return new ImportedServer(baseConfig(outbound), name);
    }

    private static ImportedServer fromSocks(String link) throws Exception {
        Uri uri = Uri.parse(link);
        String address = uri.getHost();
        int port = uri.getPort() < 1 ? 1080 : uri.getPort();
        if (address == null || address.trim().isEmpty() || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("The SOCKS link needs a valid host and port.");
        }
        String fragment = uri.getFragment();
        String name = fragment == null || fragment.trim().isEmpty()
            ? "SOCKS5 server"
            : Uri.decode(fragment).trim();
        JSONObject serverObj = new JSONObject()
            .put("address", address.trim())
            .put("port", port);
        String userInfo = uri.getUserInfo();
        if (userInfo != null && !userInfo.trim().isEmpty()) {
            String decoded = userInfo.contains(":") ? userInfo : decodeBase64Lenient(userInfo);
            int sep = decoded.indexOf(':');
            if (sep > 0) {
                String user = Uri.decode(decoded.substring(0, sep));
                String pass = Uri.decode(decoded.substring(sep + 1));
                serverObj.put("users", new JSONArray().put(
                    new JSONObject().put("user", user).put("pass", pass)
                ));
            }
        }
        JSONObject outbound = new JSONObject()
            .put("tag", "proxy")
            .put("protocol", "socks")
            .put("settings", new JSONObject().put("servers", new JSONArray().put(serverObj)))
            .put("streamSettings", new JSONObject()
                .put("network", "tcp")
                .put("security", "none")
                .put("sockopt", stabilizedSockopt()));
        return new ImportedServer(baseConfig(outbound), name);
    }

    private static String normalizeNetwork(String raw) {
        if (raw == null || raw.trim().isEmpty()) return "tcp";
        String lower = raw.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if ("websocket".equals(lower)) return "ws";
        if ("h2".equals(lower) || "http2".equals(lower)) return "http";
        if ("mkcp".equals(lower)) return "kcp";
        if ("splithttp".equals(lower)) return "xhttp";
        return lower;
    }

    private static String normalizeSecurity(String raw, boolean defaultTls) {
        if (raw == null || raw.trim().isEmpty() || "0".equals(raw.trim()) || "false".equalsIgnoreCase(raw.trim())) {
            return defaultTls ? "tls" : "none";
        }
        String lower = raw.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if ("1".equals(lower) || "true".equals(lower) || "xtls".equals(lower)) return "tls";
        return lower;
    }

    private static JSONObject stabilizedSockopt() throws Exception {
        return new JSONObject()
            .put("tcpKeepAliveIdle", 120)
            .put("tcpNoDelay", true);
    }

    private static JSONObject streamSettings(String network, String security, String address, JSONObject params) throws Exception {
        if (!Arrays.asList("tcp", "ws", "grpc", "http", "httpupgrade", "xhttp", "kcp").contains(network)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported transport: " + network);
        }
        if (!Arrays.asList("none", "tls", "reality").contains(security)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported security: " + security);
        }
        JSONObject stream = new JSONObject()
            .put("network", network)
            .put("security", security)
            .put("sockopt", stabilizedSockopt());
        if ("tls".equals(security)) {
            String sni = params.optString("sni", params.optString("host", address)).trim();
            if (sni.isEmpty()) sni = address;
            String fp = params.optString("fp", "chrome").trim();
            if (fp.isEmpty()) fp = "chrome";
            JSONObject tls = new JSONObject()
                .put("serverName", sni)
                .put("fingerprint", fp);
            String insecure = params.optString("allowInsecure", params.optString("insecure", "0"));
            if ("1".equals(insecure) || "true".equalsIgnoreCase(insecure)) {
                tls.put("allowInsecure", true);
            }
            if (params.has("alpn")) {
                String alpnRaw = params.getString("alpn");
                JSONArray alpnArr = new JSONArray();
                for (String token : alpnRaw.split(",")) {
                    String trimmed = token.trim();
                    if (!trimmed.isEmpty()) alpnArr.put(trimmed);
                }
                if (alpnArr.length() > 0) tls.put("alpn", alpnArr);
            }
            stream.put("tlsSettings", tls);
        } else if ("reality".equals(security)) {
            String publicKey = params.optString("pbk").trim();
            if (publicKey.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Reality requires a public key (pbk).");
            String sni = params.optString("sni", params.optString("host", address)).trim();
            if (sni.isEmpty()) sni = address;
            String fp = params.optString("fp", "chrome").trim();
            if (fp.isEmpty()) fp = "chrome";
            stream.put("realitySettings", new JSONObject()
                .put("serverName", sni)
                .put("fingerprint", fp)
                .put("publicKey", publicKey)
                .put("shortId", params.optString("sid", "").trim())
                .put("spiderX", params.optString("spx", "/").trim()));
        }
        if ("ws".equals(network)) {
            String wsPath = params.optString("path", "/").trim();
            if (wsPath.isEmpty()) wsPath = "/";
            JSONObject ws = new JSONObject().put("path", wsPath);
            if (params.has("host") && !params.optString("host").trim().isEmpty()) {
                ws.put("headers", new JSONObject().put("Host", params.getString("host").trim()));
            }
            stream.put("wsSettings", ws);
        } else if ("grpc".equals(network)) {
            stream.put("grpcSettings", new JSONObject()
                .put("serviceName", params.optString("serviceName", params.optString("path", "")))
                .put("multiMode", "multi".equalsIgnoreCase(params.optString("mode")))
                .put("idle_timeout", 60)
                .put("health_check_timeout", 20));
        } else if ("http".equals(network)) {
            String host = params.optString("host", address);
            stream.put("httpSettings", new JSONObject()
                .put("path", params.optString("path", "/"))
                .put("host", new JSONArray(host.split(","))));
        } else if ("httpupgrade".equals(network) || "xhttp".equals(network)) {
            stream.put(network + "Settings", new JSONObject()
                .put("path", params.optString("path", "/"))
                .put("host", params.optString("host", address)));
        } else if ("kcp".equals(network)) {
            String headerType = params.optString("headerType", params.optString("type", "none"));
            stream.put("kcpSettings", new JSONObject()
                .put("mtu", 1350)
                .put("tti", 50)
                .put("header", new JSONObject().put("type", headerType)));
        }
        return stream;
    }

    private static JSONObject baseConfig(JSONObject proxy) throws Exception {
        return new JSONObject()
            .put("log", new JSONObject().put("loglevel", "none"))
            .put("dns", new JSONObject().put("servers", new JSONArray().put("1.1.1.1").put("8.8.8.8")))
            .put("inbounds", new JSONArray()
                .put(new JSONObject().put("tag", "socks").put("listen", "127.0.0.1").put("port", 10808)
                    .put("protocol", "socks").put("settings", new JSONObject().put("udp", true)))
                .put(new JSONObject().put("tag", "http").put("listen", "127.0.0.1").put("port", 10809)
                    .put("protocol", "http").put("settings", new JSONObject())))
            .put("outbounds", new JSONArray()
                .put(proxy)
                .put(new JSONObject().put("tag", "direct").put("protocol", "freedom").put("settings", new JSONObject()))
                .put(new JSONObject().put("tag", "block").put("protocol", "blackhole").put("settings", new JSONObject())))
            .put("routing", new JSONObject()
                .put("domainStrategy", "IPIfNonMatch")
                .put("rules", new JSONArray().put(new JSONObject()
                    .put("type", "field")
                    .put("ip", new JSONArray()
                        .put("10.0.0.0/8")
                        .put("172.16.0.0/12")
                        .put("192.168.0.0/16")
                        .put("127.0.0.0/8")
                        .put("169.254.0.0/16")
                        .put("::1/128")
                        .put("fc00::/7")
                        .put("fe80::/10"))
                    .put("outboundTag", "direct"))));
    }

    private static String query(Uri uri, String key, String fallback) {
        String value = uri.getQueryParameter(key);
        return value == null || value.trim().isEmpty() ? fallback : value;
    }

    private static void requireEndpoint(String address, String credential, int port) {
        if (address == null || address.trim().isEmpty() || credential == null || credential.trim().isEmpty() || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("The server link needs a host, credential and valid port.");
        }
    }

    private static int integer(String value, int fallback) {
        try {
            return Integer.parseInt(value.trim());
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            return fallback;
        }
    }

    private static String decodeBase64Lenient(String value) {
        try {
            return decodeBase64(value);
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            return value;
        }
    }

    private static String decodeBase64(String value) {
        String normalized = value.replace('-', '+').replace('_', '/').replaceAll("\\s", "");
        int padding = (4 - normalized.length() % 4) % 4;
        for (int i = 0; i < padding; i++) normalized += "=";
        try {
            return new String(Base64.decode(normalized, Base64.DEFAULT), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException error) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("The configuration link contains invalid Base64 data.", error);
        }
    }

    static final class ImportedServer {
        final JSONObject config;
        final String name;

        ImportedServer(JSONObject config, String name) {
            this.config = config;
            this.name = name;
        }
    }
}
