}

@Composable
private fun SettingsScreen(
    dns: String,
    onDnsChange: (String) -> Unit,
    route: String,
    onRouteChange: (String) -> Unit,
    protocols: Set<String>,
    onProtocolsChange: (Set<String>) -> Unit,
    batteryOptimizationIgnored: Boolean,
    onToggleBatteryOptimization: (Boolean) -> Unit,
    onOpenVpnSettings: () -> Unit,
    onExport: () -> Unit,
) {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
    ) {
        SectionTitle("TUNNEL & PROTOCOL MATRIX")
        MetalPanel(compact = true) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Text(
                        "DNS RESOLVER",
                        color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                        fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                        fontSize = 9.5.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    )
                    Text(
                        dns,
                        color = CyanGauge,
                        fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                        fontSize = 9.5.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    )
                }
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                    listOf("1.1.1.1", "8.8.8.8", "94.140.14.14", "9.9.9.9").forEach { addr ->
                        Keycap(
                            label = addr,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            selected = dns == addr,
                        ) { onDnsChange(addr) }
                    }
                }

                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(1.dp).background(NetconColors.BevelBorder))

                Text(
                    "ALLOWED PROTOCOLS",
                    color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                    fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                    fontSize = 9.5.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                )
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                ) {
                    listOf(
                        "VLESS" to "vless",
                        "VMESS" to "vmess",
                        "TROJAN" to "trojan",
                        "SS" to "shadowsocks",
                    ).forEach { (name, id) ->
                        val checked = id in protocols
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .heightIn(min = 36.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(7.dp))
                                .background(
                                    if (checked) GoldAccent.copy(alpha = 0.2f) else Color(0xFF0D1722),
                                )
                                .border(
                                    1.dp,
                                    if (checked) GoldText else Color(0xFF2C3E52),
                                    RoundedCornerShape(7.dp),
                                )
                                .clickable {
                                    onProtocolsChange(if (checked) protocols - id else protocols + id)
                                }
                                .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
                        ) {
                            Box(
                                Modifier
                                    .size(6.dp)
                                    .background(
                                        if (checked) EmeraldOnline else Color(0xFF475569),
                                        CircleShape,
                                    ),
                            )
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(5.dp))
                            Text(
                                name,
                                color = if (checked) NetconColors.CopperLight else NetconColors.TextMuted,
                                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                                fontSize = 9.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(1.dp).background(NetconColors.BevelBorder))

                Text(
                    "ROUTING MODE",
                    color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                    fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                    fontSize = 9.5.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                )
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                    listOf("Global", "Bypass LAN", "Rule-based").forEach { value ->
                        Keycap(
                            label = value,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            selected = route == value,
                        ) { onRouteChange(value) }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        SectionTitle("BACKGROUND TUNNEL SURVIVABILITY")
        MetalPanel(compact = true) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                ) {
                    Text(
                        "VPNSERVICE POWER EXEMPTION",
                        color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                        fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                        fontSize = 9.5.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    )
                    val badgeColor = if (batteryOptimizationIgnored) EmeraldOnline else GoldText
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                            .background(badgeColor.copy(alpha = 0.14f))
                            .border(1.dp, badgeColor.copy(alpha = 0.55f), RoundedCornerShape(4.dp))
                            .padding(horizontal = 7.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            if (batteryOptimizationIgnored) "UNRESTRICTED" else "OPTIMIZED",
                            color = badgeColor,
                            fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                            fontSize = 8.5.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        )
                    }
                }

                Row(
                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                ) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            "Battery Optimization Ignore",
                            color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 12.5.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        )
                        Text(
                            if (batteryOptimizationIgnored) {
                                "NetconVpnService (:vpn) protected from Doze"
                            } else {
                                "Prevent Android from killing background tunnel"
                            },
                            color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                            fontSize = 10.sp,
                            maxLines = 1,
                            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                    Switch(
                        checked = batteryOptimizationIgnored,
                        onCheckedChange = onToggleBatteryOptimization,
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("battery_optimization_switch"),
                        colors = SwitchDefaults.colors(
                            checkedThumbColor = Color(0xFF1D0E05),
                            checkedTrackColor = EmeraldOnline,
                            uncheckedThumbColor = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                            uncheckedTrackColor = NetconColors.ChassisDark,
                            uncheckedBorderColor = NetconColors.BevelBorder,
                        ),
                    )
                }

                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp),
                ) {
                    CompactMetricTile(
                        label = "TARGET SERVICE",
                        value = "NetconVpnService (:vpn)",
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    CompactMetricTile(
                        label = "DOZE GUARD",
                        value = if (batteryOptimizationIgnored) "IGNORE ACTIVE" else "SYSTEM MANAGED",
                        valueColor = if (batteryOptimizationIgnored) EmeraldOnline else GoldText,
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                }

                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(1.dp).background(NetconColors.BevelBorder))

                Row(
                    Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                ) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            "Android VPN Lockdown",
                            color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        )
                        Text(
                            "Always-on VPN & block non-VPN traffic",
                            color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                            fontSize = 10.sp,
                        )
                    }
                    SecondaryActionButton(label = "OPEN", onClick = onOpenVpnSettings)
                }
            }
        }

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
        ) {
            CopperButton(
                label = "EXPORT JSON",
                icon = Icons.Filled.ContentCopy,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .testTag("export_json_button"),
                onClick = onExport,
            )
            SecondaryActionButton(
                label = "VPN SETTINGS",
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                onClick = onOpenVpnSettings,
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun LicenseActivationDialog(
    licenseStatus: NetconLicenseManager.LicenseStatus,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    onActivateLicenseKey: (String, String) -> NetconLicenseManager.ActivationResult,
    onSimulateTrialExpired: () -> Unit,
    onResetTrialWindow: () -> Unit,
) {
    var accountInput by rememberSaveable {
        mutableStateOf(
            licenseStatus.activatedAccount.ifBlank { NetconLicenseManager.DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT },
        )
    }
    var keyInput by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var feedbackMsg by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var feedbackError by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                .background(NetconColors.PanelSurface)
                .border(1.dp, GoldText.copy(alpha = 0.65f), RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                .padding(16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        ) {
            Row(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Text(
                    "1-KEY-PER-ACCOUNT LICENSE",
                    color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
                    fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                    fontSize = 13.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                )
                IconButton(onClick = onDismiss, modifier = Modifier.minimumInteractiveComponentSize()) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "Close", tint = NetconColors.TextSecondary)
                }
            }

            Text(
                text = if (licenseStatus.isLicensed) {
                    "Bound to ${licenseStatus.activatedAccount} (${licenseStatus.activatedKeyMasked}). Each Netcon key works exclusively for its assigned account."
                } else {
                    "3-day (72-hour) free trial (${licenseStatus.formatRemainingCountdown()} remaining). Each activation key is cryptographically bound to one account email and refuses activation on any other account."
                },
                color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                fontSize = 11.sp,
            )

            RecessedInput(
                value = accountInput,
                onValueChange = { accountInput = it },
                placeholder = "Account email (e.g. arxdevmm@gmail.com)",
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("dialog_account_input"),
            )

            RecessedInput(
                value = keyInput,
                onValueChange = { keyInput = it },
                placeholder = "NETCON-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX",
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("dialog_license_input"),
            )

            if (feedbackMsg.isNotBlank()) {
                Text(
                    text = feedbackMsg,
                    color = if (feedbackError) Color(0xFFFCA5A5) else Color(0xFF4ADE80),
                    fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                )
            }

            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                SecondaryActionButton(
                    label = "ISSUE FOR EMAIL",
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    onClick = {
                        val targetAccount = accountInput.trim().ifBlank { NetconLicenseManager.DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT }
                        accountInput = targetAccount
                        keyInput = NetconLicenseManager.generateLicenseKeyForAccount(targetAccount, "FULL", "2026")
                        feedbackError = false
                        feedbackMsg = "Generated 1-account key locked to $targetAccount. Tap ACTIVATE KEY."
                    },
                )
                CopperButton(
                    label = "ACTIVATE KEY",
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .testTag("dialog_activate_key_button"),
                    onClick = {
                        val res = onActivateLicenseKey(accountInput, keyInput)
                        feedbackError = !res.success
                        feedbackMsg = res.message
                    },
                )
            }

            Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(1.dp).background(NetconColors.BevelBorder))

            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                SecondaryActionButton(
                    label = "TEST FREEZE",
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .testTag("test_freeze_button"),
                    onClick = onSimulateTrialExpired,
                )
                SecondaryActionButton(
                    label = "RESET 3D TRIAL",
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    onClick = {
                        onResetTrialWindow()
                        feedbackError = false
                        feedbackMsg = "3-day (72h) trial timer reset."
                    },
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun FrozenTrialLockOverlay(
    licenseStatus: NetconLicenseManager.LicenseStatus,
    onActivateLicenseKey: (String, String) -> NetconLicenseManager.ActivationResult,
    onResetTrialWindow: () -> Unit,
) {
    var accountInput by rememberSaveable {
        mutableStateOf(NetconLicenseManager.DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT)
    }
    var keyInput by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var statusMessage by remember {
        mutableStateOf("3-day free trial expired. Enter your registered Account Email and your 1-account Netcon License Key to unlock.")
    }
    var isError by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }

    Box(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color(0xF205080E))
            .clickable(enabled = true, onClick = {})
            .testTag("frozen_trial_lock_overlay")
            .padding(18.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .widthIn(max = 460.dp)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .background(
                    Brush.verticalGradient(
                        listOf(Color(0xFF1A0F14), Color(0xFF0D131D), Color(0xFF070B11)),
                    ),
                )
                .border(1.5.dp, Color(0xFFEF4444), RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .padding(18.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(11.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .size(50.dp)
                    .clip(CircleShape)
                    .background(Color(0xFF2B0D12))
                    .border(1.5.dp, Color(0xFFEF4444), CircleShape),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
            ) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Filled.Lock,
                    contentDescription = "APK Frozen",
                    tint = Color(0xFFF87171),
                    modifier = Modifier.size(25.dp),
                )
            }

            Text(
                text = "APK FROZEN · 3-DAY TRIAL EXPIRED",
                color = Color(0xFFF87171),
                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            )

            Text(
                text = "1 KEY = 1 ACCOUNT · ALL SERVERS REFUSED",
                color = GoldText,
                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            )

            Text(
                text = statusMessage,
                color = if (isError) Color(0xFFFCA5A5) else Color(0xFF4ADE80),
                fontSize = 11.sp,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            )

            RecessedInput(
                value = accountInput,
                onValueChange = { accountInput = it },
                placeholder = "Account email (e.g. arxdevmm@gmail.com)",
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("frozen_account_input"),
            )

            RecessedInput(
                value = keyInput,
                onValueChange = { keyInput = it },
                placeholder = "Enter license key: NETCON-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX",
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("frozen_license_input"),
            )

            CopperButton(
                label = "ACTIVATE ACCOUNT-BOUND KEY",
                icon = Icons.Filled.Lock,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("frozen_activate_button"),
                onClick = {
                    val result = onActivateLicenseKey(accountInput, keyInput)
                    isError = !result.success
                    statusMessage = result.message
                },
            )

            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
            ) {
                SecondaryActionButton(
                    label = "FILL ACCOUNT KEY",
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .testTag("frozen_fill_demo_key_button"),
                    onClick = {
                        val targetAccount = accountInput.trim().ifBlank { NetconLicenseManager.DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT }
                        accountInput = targetAccount
                        keyInput = NetconLicenseManager.generateLicenseKeyForAccount(targetAccount, "FULL", "2026")
                        isError = false
                        statusMessage = "Loaded key ${keyInput} bound only to $targetAccount."
                    },
                )
                SecondaryActionButton(
                    label = "RESET 3D TRIAL",
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .testTag("frozen_reset_trial_button"),
                    onClick = onResetTrialWindow,
                )
            }

            Text(
                text = "Each key is HMAC-signed for one account email (${licenseStatus.badgeSummary()})",
                color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                fontSize = 8.5.sp,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun InfoScreen(

    licenseStatus: NetconLicenseManager.LicenseStatus,

                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                    CompactMetricTile(
                        label = if (licenseStatus.isLicensed) "BOUND ACCOUNT" else "BINDING RULE",

                        label = "TEST FREEZE",
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        onClick = { onSimulateTrialExpired(true) },
                    )
                }
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                    SecondaryActionButton(
                        label = "RESET 3D TRIAL",
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        onClick = onResetTrialWindow,
                    )
                    SecondaryActionButton(
                        label = "SYSTEM VPN",
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                        onClick = onOpenVpnSettings,
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        SectionTitle("CORE TELEMETRY & RELEASE BUILD")
        MetalPanel(compact = true) {
            Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                    CompactMetricTile(

                        label = "TUNNEL STATUS",

                    CompactMetricTile(
                        label = "ACTIVE NODE",
                        value = serverName.ifBlank { "—" },
                        modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                    )
                }
                Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                    CompactMetricTile(
                        label = "RELEASE CI",

                        label = "SIGNING KEY",

@Composable
private fun PublicServerDialog(
    initialCountryCode: String,
    activeServerName: String = "",
    onDismiss: () -> Unit,
    fetchNodes: suspend (String) -> NativePublicDirectory.Result,
    onSelectNode: (NativePublicDirectory.Node) -> Unit,
    onSelectPreset: (NativeXrayConfig.ServerProfilePreset) -> Unit = {},
    onImport: (String) -> Unit,
) {
    var raw by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var countryCode by rememberSaveable(initialCountryCode) { mutableStateOf(initialCountryCode) }
    var search by rememberSaveable { mutableStateOf("") }
    var countryMenu by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var loading by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var error by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var nodes by remember { mutableStateOf(emptyList<NativePublicDirectory.Node>()) }
    val presets = remember { NativeXrayConfig.presetProfiles() }
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    fun refresh() {
        if (loading) return
        loading = true
        error = ""
        scope.launch {
            try {
                val result = withTimeout(25_000) { fetchNodes(countryCode) }
                nodes = result.nodes
                error = "${result.nodes.size} supported · ${result.skipped} skipped"
            } catch (cause: Exception) {
                error = cause.message ?: "Public directory unavailable. Try another feed."
            } finally {
                loading = false
            }
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(countryCode) { refresh() }

    val visiblePresets = presets.filter {
        (countryCode.isEmpty() || it.countryCode == countryCode) &&
            (search.isBlank() || "${it.name} ${it.countryName} ${it.protocol} ${it.transportSummary}".contains(search, ignoreCase = true))
    }

    val visibleNodes = nodes.filter {
        search.isBlank() || "${it.name} ${it.country} ${it.protocol}".contains(search, ignoreCase = true)
    }.take(40)

    Dialog(onDismissRequest = onDismiss) {
        Column(
            Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                .background(NetconColors.PanelSurface)
                .border(1.dp, NetconColors.BevelHighlight, RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                .padding(16.dp)
                .heightIn(max = 680.dp)
                .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
        ) {
            Row(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Text(
                    "SERVER PROFILES & DIRECTORY",
                    color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
                    fontSize = 15.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                )
                IconButton(onClick = onDismiss, modifier = Modifier.minimumInteractiveComponentSize()) {
                    Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = "Close", tint = NetconColors.TextSecondary)
                }
            }

            if (visiblePresets.isNotEmpty()) {
                Text(
                    "STABILIZED MULTI-PROTOCOL PROFILES",
                    color = NetconColors.CopperLight,
                    fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                )
                visiblePresets.forEach { preset ->
                    val isSelected = preset.name == activeServerName
                    Row(
                        Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                            .background(if (isSelected) Color(0xFF122638) else NetconColors.ChassisDark)
                            .border(
                                1.dp,
                                if (isSelected) NetconColors.CyberCyan else NetconColors.BevelBorder,
                                RoundedCornerShape(8.dp),
                            )
                            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 9.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    ) {
                        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                "${preset.countryCode} · ${preset.name}",
                                color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
                                fontSize = 12.sp,
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            )
                            Text(
                                "${preset.transportSummary} · ${preset.cityLabel}",
                                color = NetconColors.CyberCyan,
                                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                                fontSize = 9.sp,
                            )
                        }
                        CopperButton(
                            label = if (isSelected) "ACTIVE" else "USE",
                            onClick = { onSelectPreset(preset) },
                        )
                    }
                }
                Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(1.dp).background(NetconColors.BevelBorder))
            }

            Text(
                "FREE PUBLIC SERVERS · FASTNODES COMMUNITY FEED",
                color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            )

            Box {
                Keycap(
                    label = if (countryCode.isEmpty()) "TOP FEED · ALL REGIONS"
                    else "${Locale("", countryCode).displayCountry.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)} ($countryCode)",
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    selected = countryCode.isNotEmpty(),
                    onClick = { countryMenu = true },
                )
                DropdownMenu(expanded = countryMenu, onDismissRequest = { countryMenu = false }) {
                    DropdownMenuItem(
                        text = { Text("Top feed · All regions") },
                        onClick = {
                            countryCode = ""
                            countryMenu = false
                        },
                    )
                    NativePublicDirectory.countryCodes.forEach { code ->
                        DropdownMenuItem(
                            text = { Text("${Locale("", code).displayCountry} ($code)") },
                            onClick = {
                                countryCode = code
                                countryMenu = false
                            },
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            RecessedInput(search, { search = it }, "Search protocol or location")

            Row(
                Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            ) {
                Text(
                    if (loading) "Loading public feed…" else error,
                    color = if (loading) NetconColors.CopperLight else NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                    fontSize = 10.sp,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                )
                Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                SecondaryActionButton(
                    label = "REFRESH",
                    icon = Icons.Filled.Refresh,
                    onClick = ::refresh,
                )
            }

            visibleNodes.forEach { node ->
                Row(
                    Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .background(NetconColors.ChassisDark)
                        .border(1.dp, NetconColors.BevelBorder, RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 9.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                ) {
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text(
                            node.name,
                            color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
                            fontSize = 12.sp,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        )
                        Text(
                            "${node.protocol.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)} · ${node.country}",
                            color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                            fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                            fontSize = 9.sp,
                        )
                    }
                    CopperButton("IMPORT", onClick = { onSelectNode(node) })
                }
            }

            if (nodes.isEmpty() && !loading && error.isNotBlank()) {
                Text(
                    "Try refreshing, picking another country, or pasting a link below.",
                    color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                )
            }

            Text(
                "PASTE A SERVER LINK",
                color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
                fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
                fontSize = 10.sp,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            )
            RecessedInput(raw, { raw = it }, "vless://, vmess://, trojan://, ss:// or Xray JSON", minLines = 3)
            Row(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                SecondaryActionButton("CANCEL", modifier = Modifier.weight(1f), onClick = onDismiss)
                CopperButton("IMPORT LOCALLY", Modifier.weight(1f)) { onImport(raw.trim()) }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CompactMetricTile(
    label: String,
    value: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    valueColor: Color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(7.dp))
            .background(Color(0xFF070C14))
            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF263447), RoundedCornerShape(7.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp),
    ) {
        Text(
            label,
            color = NetconColors.TextSecondary,
            fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
            fontSize = 7.5.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
        )
        Text(
            value,
            color = valueColor,
            fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
            fontSize = 10.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MetalPanel(
    compact: Boolean = false,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit,
) {
    Column(
        Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(11.dp))
            .background(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(ServerNavyTop, ServerNavyBottom),
                ),
            )
            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF2D3B50), RoundedCornerShape(11.dp))
            .padding(if (compact) 10.dp else 14.dp),
    ) {
        content()
    }
}

@Composable
private fun RecessedInput(
    value: String,
    onValueChange: (String) -> Unit,
    placeholder: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    minLines: Int = 1,
    secure: Boolean = false,
) {
    Box(
        modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .background(Color(0xFF060A10))
            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF2D3C50), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 11.dp),
    ) {
        if (value.isEmpty()) {
            Text(
                placeholder,
                color = NetconColors.TextMuted,
                fontSize = 12.sp,
                maxLines = 1,
                overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            )
        }
        BasicTextField(
            value = value,
            onValueChange = onValueChange,
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            textStyle = TextStyle(
                color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
                fontFamily = SpaceGroteskFont,
                fontSize = 13.sp,
            ),
            minLines = minLines,
            cursorBrush = Brush.verticalGradient(listOf(NetconColors.CopperLight, GoldAccent)),
            visualTransformation = if (secure) PasswordVisualTransformation() else VisualTransformation.None,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun Keycap(
    label: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    selected: Boolean = false,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    Box(
        modifier
            .heightIn(min = 34.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(7.dp))
            .background(
                if (selected) GoldAccent.copy(alpha = 0.24f) else Color(0xFF0D1722),
            )
            .border(
                1.dp,
                if (selected) GoldText else Color(0xFF2C3E52),
                RoundedCornerShape(7.dp),
            )
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center,
    ) {
        Text(
            label,
            color = if (selected) NetconColors.CopperLight else NetconColors.TextSecondary,
            fontSize = 9.5.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CopperButton(
    label: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    icon: ImageVector? = null,
    onClick: () -> Unit,
) {
    Row(
        modifier
            .heightIn(min = 38.dp)
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .background(
                Brush.verticalGradient(
                    listOf(Color(0xFFF6D08A), Color(0xFFD9822B), Color(0xFF9C5114)),
                ),
            )
            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFFFBE0A9).copy(alpha = 0.5f), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
    ) {
        if (icon != null) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = icon,
                contentDescription = null,
                tint = Color(0xFF1D0E05),
                modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp),
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.width(5.dp))
        }
        Text(
            label,
            color = Color(0xFF1D0E05),
            fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
            fontSize = 9.5.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            maxLines = 1,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SecondaryActionButton(
    label: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,

            .border(1.dp, Color(0xFF3E4E68), RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
            .clickable(onClick = onClick)
            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 8.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
    ) {
        if (icon != null) {
            Icon(
                imageVector = icon,
                contentDescription = null,

                modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp),

            Spacer(Modifier.width(5.dp))
        }
        Text(
            label,
            color = NetconColors.TextPrimary,
            fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
            fontSize = 9.5.sp,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            maxLines = 1,
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SectionTitle(text: String) {
    Text(
        text,
        color = DarkInk,
        fontFamily = JetBrainsMonoFont,
        fontSize = 9.5.sp,
        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
        letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
    )
}

private fun formatBytes(bytes: Long): String = when {
    bytes < 1000 -> "$bytes B"
    bytes < 1_000_000 -> "%.2f KB".format(bytes / 1000.0)
    bytes < 1_000_000_000 -> "%.2f MB".format(bytes / 1_000_000.0)
    else -> "%.2f GB".format(bytes / 1_000_000_000.0)
}

package com.artesys.netcon

import android.content.Context

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.UUID
import javax.crypto.Mac
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec
import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min

/**
 * Enforces the 3-day (72-hour) free trial and 1-Key-Per-1-Account HMAC-SHA256 license verification.
 *
 * How 1-Key-Per-1-Account Binding Works:
 * 1. Each issued license key (`NETCON-<ACCT>-<PLAN>-<YEAR>-<SIG>`) is cryptographically bound to
 *    a single normalized account email/ID (`accountIdentifier`):
 *    - `<ACCT>` (Block 1) is the 4-char HMAC-SHA256 fingerprint of `"ACCT:<normalizedAccount>"`.
 *    - `<SIG>` (Block 4) is the 4-char HMAC-SHA256 signature of `"<normalizedAccount>|<ACCT>-<PLAN>-<YEAR>"`.
 * 2. A key generated for `userA@example.com` will fail cryptographic verification if entered by
 *    `userB@example.com`.
 * 3. In addition, the local/registry claim table binds `<normalizedKey>` to the first claiming
 *    account + installation ID (`device_install_id`), rejecting any subsequent attempt to claim
 *    the same key under a different account.
 */
object NetconLicenseManager {
    private const val PREFS_NAME = "netcon_license_prefs"
    private const val KEY_TRIAL_START_EPOCH_MS = "trial_start_epoch_ms"
    private const val KEY_MAX_SEEN_EPOCH_MS = "max_seen_epoch_ms"
    private const val KEY_ACTIVATED_LICENSE = "activated_license_key"
    private const val KEY_ACTIVATED_ACCOUNT = "activated_account_id"
    private const val KEY_ACTIVATED_AT_EPOCH_MS = "activated_at_epoch_ms"
    private const val KEY_DEVICE_INSTALL_ID = "device_install_id"
    private const val KEY_CLAIMED_REGISTRY_JSON = "claimed_key_registry_json"
    private const val KEY_SIMULATE_EXPIRED = "simulate_trial_expired"

    /** 3 days (72 hours) in milliseconds. */
    const val TRIAL_DURATION_MS: Long = 3L * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L

    const val DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT = "arxdevmm@gmail.com"
    private const val HMAC_SECRET = "NETCON-VIPER-XRAY-LICENSE-V1"
    private const val KEY_PREFIX = "NETCON"
    private const val ALPHABET = "23456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ"

    enum class State {
        TRIAL_ACTIVE,
        TRIAL_EXPIRED_FROZEN,
        LICENSED,
    }

    data class LicenseStatus(
        val state: State,
        val trialStartEpochMs: Long,
        val trialExpiresEpochMs: Long,
        val remainingTrialMs: Long,
        val activatedKeyMasked: String,
        val activatedAccount: String = "",
    ) {
        val isAccessAllowed: Boolean
            get() = state == State.LICENSED || state == State.TRIAL_ACTIVE

        val isFrozen: Boolean
            get() = state == State.TRIAL_EXPIRED_FROZEN

        val isLicensed: Boolean
            get() = state == State.LICENSED

        fun formatRemainingCountdown(): String {
            if (isLicensed) return "FULL LICENSE"
            if (remainingTrialMs <= 0L) return "00D 00H 00M"
            val totalMinutes = (remainingTrialMs + 59_999L) / 60_000L
            val days = totalMinutes / (24L * 60L)
            val hours = (totalMinutes % (24L * 60L)) / 60L
            val minutes = totalMinutes % 60L
            return String.format(Locale.US, "%dD %02dH %02dM", days, hours, minutes)
        }

        fun badgeSummary(): String = when (state) {
            State.LICENSED -> {
                val acctLabel = activatedAccount.ifBlank { "BOUND ACCOUNT" }
                "LICENSED ($acctLabel) · $activatedKeyMasked"
            }
            State.TRIAL_ACTIVE -> "FREE TRIAL · ${formatRemainingCountdown()} LEFT"
            State.TRIAL_EXPIRED_FROZEN -> "TRIAL EXPIRED · APK FROZEN"
        }
    }

    data class ActivationResult(
        val success: Boolean,
        val message: String,
        val status: LicenseStatus,
    )

    @JvmStatic
    fun isAccessAllowed(context: Context): Boolean {
        return evaluateStatus(context, System.currentTimeMillis()).isAccessAllowed
    }

    @JvmStatic
    @JvmOverloads
    fun evaluateStatus(
        context: Context,
        nowEpochMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    ): LicenseStatus {
        val prefs = prefs(context)
        return evaluateFromPreferences(
            prefs = prefs,
            packageFirstInstallMs = resolveFirstInstallTimeMs(context),
            nowEpochMs = nowEpochMs,
        )
    }

    internal fun evaluateFromPreferences(
        prefs: SharedPreferences,
        packageFirstInstallMs: Long,
        nowEpochMs: Long,
    ): LicenseStatus {
        val storedKey = prefs.getString(KEY_ACTIVATED_LICENSE, "").orEmpty().trim()
        val storedAccount = prefs.getString(KEY_ACTIVATED_ACCOUNT, "").orEmpty().trim()
        val storedStart = prefs.getLong(KEY_TRIAL_START_EPOCH_MS, 0L)
        val anchorStart = when {
            storedStart > 0L && packageFirstInstallMs > 0L -> min(storedStart, packageFirstInstallMs)
            storedStart > 0L -> storedStart
            packageFirstInstallMs > 0L -> min(nowEpochMs, packageFirstInstallMs)
            else -> nowEpochMs
        }

        val previousMaxSeen = prefs.getLong(KEY_MAX_SEEN_EPOCH_MS, anchorStart)
        val effectiveNow = max(nowEpochMs, previousMaxSeen)

        if (storedStart != anchorStart || effectiveNow > previousMaxSeen) {
            prefs.edit()
                .putLong(KEY_TRIAL_START_EPOCH_MS, anchorStart)
                .putLong(KEY_MAX_SEEN_EPOCH_MS, effectiveNow)
                .apply()
        }

        val expiresAt = anchorStart + TRIAL_DURATION_MS

        if (storedKey.isNotEmpty() && storedAccount.isNotEmpty() &&
            verifyAccountBoundLicenseKey(storedAccount, storedKey)
        ) {
            return LicenseStatus(
                state = State.LICENSED,
                trialStartEpochMs = anchorStart,
                trialExpiresEpochMs = expiresAt,
                remainingTrialMs = 0L,
                activatedKeyMasked = maskKey(storedKey),
                activatedAccount = storedAccount,
            )
        }

        val simulatedExpired = prefs.getBoolean(KEY_SIMULATE_EXPIRED, false)
        val remaining = if (simulatedExpired) 0L else max(0L, expiresAt - effectiveNow)
        val state = if (remaining > 0L) State.TRIAL_ACTIVE else State.TRIAL_EXPIRED_FROZEN

        return LicenseStatus(
            state = state,
            trialStartEpochMs = anchorStart,
            trialExpiresEpochMs = expiresAt,
            remainingTrialMs = remaining,
            activatedKeyMasked = "",
            activatedAccount = "",
        )
    }

    /**
     * Activates a license key strictly bound to [accountIdentifier] (1 Key = 1 Account).
     */
    @JvmStatic
    @JvmOverloads
    fun activateLicenseKey(
        context: Context,
        rawKey: String,
        accountIdentifier: String = DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT,
        nowEpochMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    ): ActivationResult {
        val normalizedAccount = normalizeAccount(accountIdentifier)
        val normalizedKey = normalizeLicenseKey(rawKey)
        val prefs = prefs(context)

        if (!isValidAccountIdentifier(normalizedAccount)) {
            val current = evaluateStatus(context, nowEpochMs)
            return ActivationResult(
                success = false,
                message = "Enter a valid account email (e.g. user@domain.com) to bind your license key.",
                status = current,
            )
        }

        if (!isWellFormedKey(normalizedKey)) {
            val current = evaluateStatus(context, nowEpochMs)
            return ActivationResult(
                success = false,
                message = "Invalid license key format. Expected NETCON-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX.",
                status = current,
            )
        }

        // Check single-account claim registry first
        val claimedOwner = getClaimedAccountForKey(prefs, normalizedKey)
        if (claimedOwner != null && claimedOwner != normalizedAccount) {
            val current = evaluateStatus(context, nowEpochMs)
            return ActivationResult(
                success = false,
                message = "This license key is already bound to another account ($claimedOwner). One key works for one account only.",
                status = current,
            )
        }

        // Verify cryptographic binding between normalizedAccount and normalizedKey
        if (!verifyAccountBoundLicenseKey(normalizedAccount, normalizedKey)) {
            val current = evaluateStatus(context, nowEpochMs)
            val parts = normalizedKey.split("-")
            val expectedAccountBlock = computeAccountBlock(normalizedAccount)
            val reason = if (parts.size == 5 && parts[1] != expectedAccountBlock) {
                "License key is issued for a different account (expected account tag $expectedAccountBlock for $normalizedAccount). One key works only for its assigned account."
            } else {
                "Invalid license key signature for account $normalizedAccount."
            }
            return ActivationResult(
                success = false,
                message = reason,
                status = current,
            )
        }

        recordClaimForKey(prefs, normalizedKey, normalizedAccount)

        prefs.edit()
            .putString(KEY_ACTIVATED_LICENSE, normalizedKey)
            .putString(KEY_ACTIVATED_ACCOUNT, normalizedAccount)
            .putLong(KEY_ACTIVATED_AT_EPOCH_MS, nowEpochMs)
            .putBoolean(KEY_SIMULATE_EXPIRED, false)
            .apply()

        val updated = evaluateStatus(context, nowEpochMs)
        return ActivationResult(
            success = true,
            message = "License key activated and locked to $normalizedAccount (${updated.activatedKeyMasked}).",
            status = updated,
        )
    }

    /**
     * Allows QA / user verification of the 3-day trial freeze screen without waiting 72 hours.
     */
    @JvmStatic
    fun setSimulatedTrialExpired(context: Context, expired: Boolean): LicenseStatus {
        val prefs = prefs(context)
        val editor = prefs.edit().putBoolean(KEY_SIMULATE_EXPIRED, expired)
        if (expired) {
            editor.remove(KEY_ACTIVATED_LICENSE)
            editor.remove(KEY_ACTIVATED_ACCOUNT)
        }
        editor.apply()
        return evaluateStatus(context)
    }

    /**
     * Resets the 3-day trial timer to a fresh 72-hour window and clears any activated license key.
     */
    @JvmStatic
    fun resetTrialWindow(context: Context, nowEpochMs: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): LicenseStatus {
        prefs(context).edit()
            .putLong(KEY_TRIAL_START_EPOCH_MS, nowEpochMs)
            .putLong(KEY_MAX_SEEN_EPOCH_MS, nowEpochMs)
            .remove(KEY_ACTIVATED_LICENSE)
            .remove(KEY_ACTIVATED_ACCOUNT)
            .remove(KEY_ACTIVATED_AT_EPOCH_MS)
            .putBoolean(KEY_SIMULATE_EXPIRED, false)
            .apply()
        return evaluateFromPreferences(
            prefs = prefs(context),
            packageFirstInstallMs = nowEpochMs,
            nowEpochMs = nowEpochMs,
        )
    }

    /**
     * Verifies that [rawKey] (`NETCON-AAAA-PPPP-YYYY-SSSS`) is cryptographically bound to
     * [accountIdentifier], where `AAAA` is the 4-char HMAC fingerprint of [accountIdentifier]
     * and `SSSS` is the 4-char HMAC signature over `"<normalizedAccount>|AAAA-PPPP-YYYY"`.
     */
    @JvmStatic
    fun verifyAccountBoundLicenseKey(accountIdentifier: String, rawKey: String): Boolean {
        val normalizedAccount = normalizeAccount(accountIdentifier)
        if (!isValidAccountIdentifier(normalizedAccount)) return false
        val normalizedKey = normalizeLicenseKey(rawKey)
        if (!isWellFormedKey(normalizedKey)) return false

        val parts = normalizedKey.split("-")
        val g1 = parts[1]
        val g2 = parts[2]
        val g3 = parts[3]
        val sig = parts[4]

        val expectedAccountBlock = computeAccountBlock(normalizedAccount)
        if (g1 != expectedAccountBlock) return false

        val expectedSig = computeAccountSignatureBlock(normalizedAccount, g1, g2, g3)
        return sig == expectedSig
    }

    /**
     * Legacy helper that verifies against [DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT].
     */
    @JvmStatic
    fun verifyLicenseKeyFormatAndSignature(rawKey: String): Boolean {
        return verifyAccountBoundLicenseKey(DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT, rawKey)
    }

    /**
     * Generates a 1-Key-Per-1-Account signed Netcon license key (`NETCON-AAAA-PPPP-YYYY-SSSS`)
     * locked to [accountIdentifier].
     */
    @JvmStatic
    @JvmOverloads
    fun generateLicenseKeyForAccount(
        accountIdentifier: String,
        planBlock: String = "FULL",
        yearBlock: String = "2026",
    ): String {
        val normalizedAccount = normalizeAccount(accountIdentifier)
        val b1 = computeAccountBlock(normalizedAccount)
        val b2 = sanitizeBlock(planBlock)
        val b3 = sanitizeBlock(yearBlock)
        val sig = computeAccountSignatureBlock(normalizedAccount, b1, b2, b3)
        return "$KEY_PREFIX-$b1-$b2-$b3-$sig"
    }

    /**
     * Returns a deterministic official verification key bound to [DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT]
     * (`arxdevmm@gmail.com`).
     */
    @JvmStatic
    fun defaultVerificationKey(): String = generateLicenseKeyForAccount(DEFAULT_DEMO_ACCOUNT, "FULL", "2026")

    internal fun computeAccountBlock(normalizedAccount: String): String {
        return hmacToFourChars("ACCT:$normalizedAccount")
    }

    internal fun computeAccountSignatureBlock(
        normalizedAccount: String,
        block1: String,
        block2: String,
        block3: String,
    ): String {
        val payload = "$normalizedAccount|${block1.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)}-${block2.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)}-${block3.uppercase(Locale.ROOT)}"
        return hmacToFourChars(payload)
    }

    private fun hmacToFourChars(payload: String): String {
        val mac = Mac.getInstance("HmacSHA256")
        mac.init(SecretKeySpec(HMAC_SECRET.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), "HmacSHA256"))
        val digest = mac.doFinal(payload.toByteArray(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))
        val chars = CharArray(4)
        for (i in 0 until 4) {
            val index = (digest[i].toInt() and 0xFF) % ALPHABET.length
            chars[i] = ALPHABET[index]
        }
        return String(chars)
    }

    internal fun normalizeAccount(rawAccount: String): String {
        return rawAccount.trim().lowercase(Locale.ROOT)
    }

    private fun isValidAccountIdentifier(normalizedAccount: String): Boolean {
        return normalizedAccount.length >= 3 && !normalizedAccount.contains(" ")
    }

    private fun isWellFormedKey(normalizedKey: String): Boolean {
        val parts = normalizedKey.split("-")
        if (parts.size != 5) return false
        if (parts[0] != KEY_PREFIX) return false
        return parts.drop(1).all { it.length == 4 && isAlphaNumericBlock(it) }
    }

    private fun normalizeLicenseKey(rawKey: String): String {
        return rawKey.trim()
            .uppercase(Locale.ROOT)
            .replace(" ", "-")
    }

    private fun sanitizeBlock(raw: String): String {
        val cleaned = raw.uppercase(Locale.ROOT).filter { it.isLetterOrDigit() }
        return cleaned.padEnd(4, 'X').take(4)
    }

    private fun isAlphaNumericBlock(block: String): Boolean {
        return block.all { it in '0'..'9' || it in 'A'..'Z' }
    }

    private fun maskKey(normalizedKey: String): String {
        val parts = normalizedKey.split("-")
        return if (parts.size == 5) {
            "${parts[0]}-${parts[1]}-••••-${parts[3]}-${parts[4]}"
        } else {
            normalizedKey
        }
    }

    private fun getClaimedAccountForKey(prefs: SharedPreferences, normalizedKey: String): String? {
        val rawJson = prefs.getString(KEY_CLAIMED_REGISTRY_JSON, "{}").orEmpty()
        return try {
            val obj = JSONObject(rawJson)
            obj.optString(normalizedKey, "").takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    private fun recordClaimForKey(
        prefs: SharedPreferences,
        normalizedKey: String,
        normalizedAccount: String,
    ) {
        val installId = prefs.getString(KEY_DEVICE_INSTALL_ID, null) ?: UUID.randomUUID().toString()
        val rawJson = prefs.getString(KEY_CLAIMED_REGISTRY_JSON, "{}").orEmpty()
        val obj = try {
            JSONObject(rawJson)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            JSONObject()
        }
        obj.put(normalizedKey, normalizedAccount)
        prefs.edit()
            .putString(KEY_DEVICE_INSTALL_ID, installId)
            .putString(KEY_CLAIMED_REGISTRY_JSON, obj.toString())
            .apply()
    }

    private fun prefs(context: Context): SharedPreferences {
        return context.applicationContext.getSharedPreferences(PREFS_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    }

    @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
    private fun resolveFirstInstallTimeMs(context: Context): Long {
        return try {
            val pm = context.packageManager
            val pkgInfo = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
                pm.getPackageInfo(context.packageName, android.content.pm.PackageManager.PackageInfoFlags.of(0))
            } else {
                pm.getPackageInfo(context.packageName, 0)
            }
            pkgInfo.firstInstallTime.coerceAtLeast(0L)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            0L
        }
    }
}
