==> ./app/src/test/java/com/artesys/netcon/NativeXrayConfigTest.java <==
package com.artesys.netcon;

import static org.junit.Assert.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.Assert.assertTrue;

import org.junit.Test;
import org.junit.runner.RunWith;
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner;
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config;

import java.util.List;

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner.class)
@Config(manifest = Config.NONE)
public final class NativeXrayConfigTest {
    @Test
    public void acceptsLocalXrayJson() throws Exception {
        NativeXrayConfig.ImportedServer imported = NativeXrayConfig.fromInput("{\"outbounds\":[{\"protocol\":\"freedom\"}]}");
        assertEquals("Imported Xray configuration", imported.name);
        assertEquals(1, imported.config.getJSONArray("outbounds").length());
    }

    @Test
    public void rejectsEmptyConfiguration() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> NativeXrayConfig.fromInput(" "));
    }

    @Test
    public void rejectsJsonWithoutProxyOutbound() {
        assertThrows(Exception.class, () -> NativeXrayConfig.fromInput("{\"inbounds\":[]}"));
    }

    @Test
    public void rejectsJsonWithEmptyOutbounds() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> NativeXrayConfig.fromInput("{\"outbounds\":[]}"));
    }

    @Test
    public void parsesVlessWithoutGeoipFileDependency() throws Exception {
        NativeXrayConfig.ImportedServer imported = NativeXrayConfig.fromInput(
            "vless://11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111@example.com:443?security=tls&type=ws&path=/ws#EdgeNode"
        );
        assertEquals("EdgeNode", imported.name);
        String json = imported.config.toString();
        assertFalse(json.contains("geoip:private"));
        String firstIp = imported.config
            .getJSONObject("routing")
            .getJSONArray("rules")
            .getJSONObject(0)
            .getJSONArray("ip")
            .getString(0);
        assertEquals("10.0.0.0/8", firstIp);
        assertEquals("direct", imported.config.getJSONArray("outbounds").getJSONObject(1).getString("tag"));
    }

    @Test
    public void parsesTrojanGrpcAndSocks5ProfilesCleanly() throws Exception {
        NativeXrayConfig.ImportedServer trojan = NativeXrayConfig.fromInput(
            "trojan://secretpass@us-edge.example.com:443?type=grpc&serviceName=gun-tun&security=tls#US-Trojan"
        );
        assertEquals("US-Trojan", trojan.name);
        assertEquals("trojan", trojan.config.getJSONArray("outbounds").getJSONObject(0).getString("protocol"));

        NativeXrayConfig.ImportedServer socks = NativeXrayConfig.fromInput(
            "socks5://user:pass@198.51.100.10:1080#GB-SocksRelay"
        );
        assertEquals("GB-SocksRelay", socks.name);
        assertEquals("socks", socks.config.getJSONArray("outbounds").getJSONObject(0).getString("protocol"));
    }

    @Test
    public void providesStabilizedMultiProtocolPresetProfiles() {
        List<NativeXrayConfig.ServerProfilePreset> presets = NativeXrayConfig.presetProfiles();
        assertTrue(presets.size() >= 6);
        assertEquals("SG-Cloudflare-Reality-01", presets.get(0).name);
        assertEquals("SG", presets.get(0).countryCode);
    }
}

==> ./app/src/test/java/com/artesys/netcon/NetconLicenseManagerTest.kt <==
package com.artesys.netcon

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
class NetconLicenseManagerTest {
    private lateinit var context: Context

    @Before
    fun setUp() {
        context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext()
        context.getSharedPreferences("netcon_license_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .edit()
            .clear()
            .commit()
    }

    @Test
    fun trialIsActiveDuringFirstThreeDaysAndFreezesAfter72Hours() {
        val startEpoch = 1_700_000_000_000L
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("netcon_license_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

        // Day 0: fresh install
        val initial = NetconLicenseManager.evaluateFromPreferences(
            prefs = prefs,
            packageFirstInstallMs = startEpoch,
            nowEpochMs = startEpoch,
        )
        assertEquals(NetconLicenseManager.State.TRIAL_ACTIVE, initial.state)
        assertTrue(initial.isAccessAllowed)
        assertFalse(initial.isFrozen)
        assertEquals(NetconLicenseManager.TRIAL_DURATION_MS, initial.remainingTrialMs)

        // 2 days + 23 hours later: still active
        val almostExpired = startEpoch + NetconLicenseManager.TRIAL_DURATION_MS - 60_000L
        val midTrial = NetconLicenseManager.evaluateFromPreferences(
            prefs = prefs,
            packageFirstInstallMs = startEpoch,
            nowEpochMs = almostExpired,
        )
        assertEquals(NetconLicenseManager.State.TRIAL_ACTIVE, midTrial.state)
        assertTrue(midTrial.isAccessAllowed)
        assertEquals(60_000L, midTrial.remainingTrialMs)

        // Exactly 72 hours (3 days) elapsed: trial expires and APK freezes
        val atExpiry = startEpoch + NetconLicenseManager.TRIAL_DURATION_MS
        val expired = NetconLicenseManager.evaluateFromPreferences(
            prefs = prefs,
            packageFirstInstallMs = startEpoch,
            nowEpochMs = atExpiry,
        )
        assertEquals(NetconLicenseManager.State.TRIAL_EXPIRED_FROZEN, expired.state)
        assertFalse(expired.isAccessAllowed)
        assertTrue(expired.isFrozen)
        assertEquals(0L, expired.remainingTrialMs)
    }

    @Test
    fun clockRollbackDoesNotExtendExpiredThreeDayTrial() {
        val startEpoch = 1_700_000_000_000L
        val prefs = context.getSharedPreferences("netcon_license_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

        val afterExpiry = startEpoch + NetconLicenseManager.TRIAL_DURATION_MS + 10_000L
        val expired = NetconLicenseManager.evaluateFromPreferences(
            prefs = prefs,
            packageFirstInstallMs = startEpoch,
            nowEpochMs = afterExpiry,
        )
        assertTrue(expired.isFrozen)

        // Attempt to roll system clock back to day 1
        val rolledBack = startEpoch + 3_600_000L
        val afterRollback = NetconLicenseManager.evaluateFromPreferences(
            prefs = prefs,
            packageFirstInstallMs = startEpoch,
            nowEpochMs = rolledBack,
        )
        assertTrue(afterRollback.isFrozen)
        assertFalse(afterRollback.isAccessAllowed)
    }

    @Test
    fun oneKeyWorksOnlyForItsAssignedAccountAndRejectsOtherAccounts() {
        val startEpoch = 1_700_000_000_000L
        NetconLicenseManager.resetTrialWindow(context, startEpoch)
        val afterExpiry = startEpoch + NetconLicenseManager.TRIAL_DURATION_MS + 5_000L

        val aliceAccount = "alice@artesys.io"
        val bobAccount = "bob@artesys.io"
        val aliceKey = NetconLicenseManager.generateLicenseKeyForAccount(aliceAccount, "FULL", "2026")

        // 1. Key generated for Alice MUST fail when Bob tries to activate it with Bob's account
        val bobAttempt = NetconLicenseManager.activateLicenseKey(
            context = context,
            rawKey = aliceKey,
            accountIdentifier = bobAccount,
            nowEpochMs = afterExpiry,
        )
        assertFalse(bobAttempt.success)
        assertTrue(bobAttempt.status.isFrozen)

        // 2. Key generated for Alice succeeds when activated with Alice's account
        val aliceAttempt = NetconLicenseManager.activateLicenseKey(
            context = context,
            rawKey = aliceKey,
            accountIdentifier = aliceAccount,
            nowEpochMs = afterExpiry,
        )
        assertTrue(aliceAttempt.success)
        assertTrue(aliceAttempt.status.isLicensed)
        assertEquals(aliceAccount, aliceAttempt.status.activatedAccount)
        assertFalse(aliceAttempt.status.isFrozen)
    }
}

==> ./scripts/manage-keystore.sh <==
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# ==============================================================================
# Netcon Android Keystore & 1-Key-Per-1-Account License Management Utility
# Compatible with app/build.gradle.kts signingConfigs.release:
#   KEYSTORE_PATH (defaults to ./my-upload-key.jks)
#   STORE_PASSWORD
#   KEY_PASSWORD
#   keyAlias = "upload"
# ==============================================================================
set -euo pipefail

KEYSTORE_FILE="${KEYSTORE_PATH:-my-upload-key.jks}"
KEY_ALIAS="upload"
HMAC_SECRET="NETCON-VIPER-XRAY-LICENSE-V1"
ALPHABET="23456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ"
DEFAULT_ACCOUNT="arxdevmm@gmail.com"

usage() {
  cat <<EOF
Usage: bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh <command> [args]

Commands:
  generate-keystore [keystore_path]
      Generates a 4096-bit RSA release keystore (default: my-upload-key.jks)
      with keyAlias="upload" matching app/build.gradle.kts.

  export-github-secrets [keystore_path]
      Outputs the base64-encoded keystore string for GitHub Actions secret
      KEYSTORE_BASE64 along with instructions for STORE_PASSWORD and KEY_PASSWORD.

  verify-apk <apk_path>
      Computes SHA-256 checksum and verifies APK signature using apksigner/jarsigner.

  generate-license-key [account_email] [PLAN] [YEAR]
      Generates a 1-Key-Per-1-Account HMAC-SHA256 signed Netcon license key
      (NETCON-AAAA-PPPP-YYYY-SSSS) cryptographically locked to <account_email>.
      A key issued for one account email will be rejected by all other accounts.

  verify-license-key <account_email> <license_key>
      Verifies whether <license_key> is valid and bound to <account_email>.

  install-workflow
      Copies scripts/release-apk.yml into .github/workflows/release-apk.yml
      for GitHub Actions automated APK release builds.
EOF
}

normalize_account() {
  local raw="${1:-$DEFAULT_ACCOUNT}"
  echo "$raw" | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]' | tr -d '[:space:]'
}

sanitize_block() {
  local raw="${1:-FULL}"
  local cleaned
  cleaned="$(echo "$raw" | tr '[:lower:]' '[:upper:]' | tr -cd 'A-Z0-9')"
  cleaned="${cleaned}XXXX"
  echo "${cleaned:0:4}"
}

hmac_to_four_chars() {
  local payload="$1"
  local bytes
  bytes="$(printf '%s' "$payload" | openssl dgst -sha256 -hmac "$HMAC_SECRET" -binary | od -An -tu1 | tr -s ' ')"
  local out=""
  local count=0
  for byte in $bytes; do
    if [ "$count" -ge 4 ]; then
      break
    fi
    local idx=$((byte % 32))
    out="${out}${ALPHABET:$idx:1}"
    count=$((count + 1))
  done
  echo "$out"
}

generate_license_key() {
  local account
  account="$(normalize_account "${1:-$DEFAULT_ACCOUNT}")"
  local b1
  local b2
  local b3
  b1="$(hmac_to_four_chars "ACCT:${account}")"
  b2="$(sanitize_block "${2:-FULL}")"
  b3="$(sanitize_block "${3:-2026}")"
  local sig
  sig="$(hmac_to_four_chars "${account}|${b1}-${b2}-${b3}")"
  echo "NETCON-${b1}-${b2}-${b3}-${sig}"
}

cmd="${1:-help}"
case "$cmd" in
  generate-keystore)
    target="${2:-$KEYSTORE_FILE}"
    if [ -f "$target" ]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Keystore already exists at: $target"
      echo "        Delete or move it first if you intend to rotate keys."
      exit 1
    fi
    STORE_PASS="${STORE_PASSWORD:-}"
    KEY_PASS="${KEY_PASSWORD:-}"
    if [ -z "$STORE_PASS" ]; then
      read -r -s -p "Enter new Keystore Password (min 6 chars): " STORE_PASS
      echo ""
    fi
    if [ -z "$KEY_PASS" ]; then
      KEY_PASS="$STORE_PASS"
    fi
    DN="${KEYSTORE_DN:-CN=Artesys Netcon Release, OU=Security Engineering, O=Artesys, L=Singapore, ST=SG, C=SG}"
    keytool -genkeypair \
      -v \
      -keystore "$target" \
      -storetype PKCS12 \
      -alias "$KEY_ALIAS" \
      -keyalg RSA \
      -keysize 4096 \
      -validity 10000 \
      -storepass "$STORE_PASS" \
      -keypass "$KEY_PASS" \
      -dname "$DN"
    chmod 600 "$target"
    echo "[OK] Generated release keystore: $target (alias: $KEY_ALIAS)"
    ;;

  export-github-secrets)
    target="${2:-$KEYSTORE_FILE}"
    if [ ! -f "$target" ]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Keystore file not found: $target"
      echo "        Run: bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh generate-keystore"
      exit 1
    fi
    b64_out="${target}.base64"
    base64 -w 0 "$target" > "$b64_out" 2>/dev/null || base64 "$target" | tr -d '\n' > "$b64_out"
    chmod 600 "$b64_out"
    echo "======================================================================"
    echo "GitHub Repository Secrets Setup (Settings -> Secrets -> Actions)"
    echo "======================================================================"
    echo "1. KEYSTORE_BASE64 : Saved to $b64_out (copy contents into secret)"
    echo "2. STORE_PASSWORD  : Your keystore password"
    echo "3. KEY_PASSWORD    : Your key password for alias '$KEY_ALIAS'"
    echo "======================================================================"
    ;;

  verify-apk)
    apk="${2:-}"
    if [ -z "$apk" ] || [ ! -f "$apk" ]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Provide a valid APK path: bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh verify-apk <path-to.apk>"
      exit 1
    fi
    sha256sum "$apk" > "${apk}.sha256"
    echo "[SHA-256] $(cat "${apk}.sha256")"
    APKSIGNER_BIN="$(command -v apksigner || true)"
    if [ -z "$APKSIGNER_BIN" ]; then
      SDK_DIR="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-/opt/android/sdk}}"
      APKSIGNER_BIN="$(find "$SDK_DIR/build-tools" -maxdepth 2 -name apksigner 2>/dev/null | sort -V | tail -n 1 || true)"
    fi
    if [ -n "$APKSIGNER_BIN" ] && [ -x "$APKSIGNER_BIN" ]; then
      "$APKSIGNER_BIN" verify --verbose --print-certs "$apk"
    elif command -v jarsigner >/dev/null 2>&1; then
      echo "[WARN] apksigner not found; falling back to jarsigner -verify"
      jarsigner -verify -verbose -certs "$apk" | tail -n 20
    else
      echo "[ERROR] Neither apksigner nor jarsigner found to verify APK signature."
      exit 1
    fi
    ;;

  sign-apk-compat)
    apk="${2:-}"
    ks="${3:-$KEYSTORE_FILE}"
    if [ -z "$apk" ] || [ ! -f "$apk" ] || [ ! -f "$ks" ]; then
      echo "[ERROR] Usage: bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh sign-apk-compat <apk_path> [keystore_path]"
      exit 1
    fi
    SDK_DIR="${ANDROID_HOME:-${ANDROID_SDK_ROOT:-/opt/android/sdk}}"
    APKSIGNER_BIN="$(command -v apksigner || find "$SDK_DIR/build-tools" -maxdepth 2 -name apksigner 2>/dev/null | sort -V | tail -n 1 || true)"
    ZIPALIGN_BIN="$(command -v zipalign || find "$SDK_DIR/build-tools" -maxdepth 2 -name zipalign 2>/dev/null | sort -V | tail -n 1 || true)"
    if [ -z "$APKSIGNER_BIN" ] || [ ! -x "$APKSIGNER_BIN" ]; then
      echo "[WARN] apksigner not found; keeping AGP v2 signature."
      exit 0
    fi
    if [ -n "$ZIPALIGN_BIN" ] && [ -x "$ZIPALIGN_BIN" ]; then
      aligned_tmp="${apk}.aligned.tmp"
      "$ZIPALIGN_BIN" -f -p 4 "$apk" "$aligned_tmp"
      mv "$aligned_tmp" "$apk"
    fi
    STORE_PASS="${STORE_PASSWORD:-}"
    KEY_PASS="${KEY_PASSWORD:-$STORE_PASS}"
    "$APKSIGNER_BIN" sign \
      --ks "$ks" \
      --ks-key-alias "$KEY_ALIAS" \
      --ks-pass "pass:${STORE_PASS}" \
      --key-pass "pass:${KEY_PASS}" \
      --min-sdk-version 21 \
      --v1-signing-enabled true \
      --v2-signing-enabled true \
      --v3-signing-enabled true \
      --v4-signing-enabled false \
      "$apk"
    rm -f "${apk}.idsig"
    echo "[OK] Signed $apk with APK Signature Schemes v1 + v2 + v3."
    ;;

  generate-license-key)
    shift || true
    acct="${1:-$DEFAULT_ACCOUNT}"
    plan="${2:-FULL}"
    year="${3:-2026}"
    generate_license_key "$acct" "$plan" "$year"
    ;;

  verify-license-key)
    shift || true
    acct="$(normalize_account "${1:-}")"
    key="$(echo "${2:-}" | tr '[:lower:]' '[:upper:]' | tr -d '[:space:]')"
    expected="$(generate_license_key "$acct" "FULL" "2026")"
    if [ "$key" = "$expected" ]; then
      echo "[VALID] Key $key is cryptographically bound to account: $acct"
    else
      echo "[REJECTED] Key $key is NOT valid for account: $acct"
      exit 1
    fi
    ;;

  install-workflow)
    mkdir -p .github/workflows
    cp scripts/release-apk.yml .github/workflows/release-apk.yml
    echo "[OK] Installed .github/workflows/release-apk.yml from scripts/release-apk.yml"
    ;;

  *)
    usage
    ;;
esac

==> ./scripts/release-apk.yml <==
name: Build, Sign & Publish Release APK

on:
  push:
    tags:
      - "v*"
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      release_tag:
        description: "Release tag (e.g. v1.0.5)"
        required: false
        default: "v1.0.5"

permissions:
  contents: write

jobs:
  release-apk:
    name: Sign & Release Android APK
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up JDK 21
        uses: actions/setup-java@v4
        with:
          distribution: "temurin"
          java-version: "21"

      - name: Setup Gradle 9.3.1
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v4
        with:
          gradle-version: "9.3.1"

      - name: Decode Release Keystore & Prepare Debug Keystore
        env:
          KEYSTORE_BASE64: ${{ secrets.KEYSTORE_BASE64 }}
          STORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.STORE_PASSWORD }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD }}
        run: |
          if [ ! -f "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/debug.keystore" ]; then
            keytool -genkeypair -v \
              -keystore "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/debug.keystore" \
              -alias androiddebugkey \
              -keyalg RSA \
              -keysize 2048 \
              -validity 10000 \
              -storepass android \
              -keypass android \
              -dname "CN=Android Debug,O=Android,C=US"
          fi
          if [ -n "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" ]; then
            echo "$KEYSTORE_BASE64" | base64 --decode > "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
            chmod 600 "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
            echo "Decoded release keystore from KEYSTORE_BASE64 secret."
          else
            echo "KEYSTORE_BASE64 not set; generating ephemeral CI keystore for build verification..."
            STORE_PASSWORD="${STORE_PASSWORD:-netcon-ci-store-pass}" \
            KEY_PASSWORD="${KEY_PASSWORD:-netcon-ci-store-pass}" \
            bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh generate-keystore "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
          fi

      - name: Run Unit Tests (3-Day Trial, License Manager & Core Logic)
        run: gradle testDebugUnitTest --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache

      - name: Build Signed Release APK
        env:
          KEYSTORE_PATH: ${{ github.workspace }}/my-upload-key.jks
          STORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.STORE_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.STORE_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.KEY_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
        run: gradle assembleRelease --no-daemon --no-configuration-cache

      - name: Prepare Release Artifacts & Checksums
        id: artifact
        env:
          STORE_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.STORE_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.STORE_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
          KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.KEY_PASSWORD != '' && secrets.KEY_PASSWORD || 'netcon-ci-store-pass' }}
        run: |
          TAG_NAME="${{ github.event.inputs.release_tag || github.ref_name }}"
          APK_SOURCE="$(find app/build/outputs/apk/release -name '*.apk' ! -name '*-unsigned.apk' | head -n 1)"
          if [ -z "$APK_SOURCE" ]; then
            echo "No signed release APK found under app/build/outputs/apk/release"
            ls -la app/build/outputs/apk/release || true
            exit 1
          fi
          OUT_APK="netcon-${TAG_NAME}-release.apk"
          cp "$APK_SOURCE" "$OUT_APK"
          bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh sign-apk-compat "$OUT_APK" "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"
          bash scripts/manage-keystore.sh verify-apk "$OUT_APK"
          echo "apk_file=${OUT_APK}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "sha_file=${OUT_APK}.sha256" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
          echo "tag_name=${TAG_NAME}" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"

      - name: Upload Signed APK Artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: netcon-release-apk
          path: |
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.apk_file }}
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.sha_file }}

      - name: Publish to GitHub Releases
        if: startsWith(github.ref, 'refs/tags/v') || github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'
        uses: softprops/action-gh-release@v2
        with:
          tag_name: ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.tag_name }}
          name: "Netcon VPN ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.tag_name }}"
          generate_release_notes: true
          body: |
            ### Netcon Multi-Protocol VPN (${{ steps.artifact.outputs.tag_name }})
            - **3-Day (72-Hour) Free Trial**: Full access to VLESS-Reality, VMess-WS-TLS, Trojan-gRPC, and Shadowsocks-2022 profiles for 72 hours from installation.
            - **License Enforcement**: After 72 hours, activate a signed Netcon license key (`NETCON-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`) to unlock unlimited connections.
            - **SHA-256 Checksum**: Verify `${{ steps.artifact.outputs.apk_file }}` against `${{ steps.artifact.outputs.sha_file }}`.
          files: |
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.apk_file }}
            ${{ steps.artifact.outputs.sha_file }}
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - name: Clean up decoded Keystore
        if: always()
        run: rm -f "${GITHUB_WORKSPACE}/my-upload-key.jks"

dependencyResolutionManagement {
  repositoriesMode.set(RepositoriesMode.FAIL_ON_PROJECT_REPOS)
  repositories {
    google()
    mavenCentral()
  }
}

abstract class EnsurePreviewServerRunning :
  org.gradle.api.provider.ValueSource<Boolean, org.gradle.api.provider.ValueSourceParameters.None> {
  override fun obtain(): Boolean {
    val isListening =
      try {
        java.net.Socket().use { socket ->
          socket.connect(java.net.InetSocketAddress("127.0.0.1", 3000), 200)
          true
        }
      } catch (_: Exception) {
        false
      }
    if (!isListening) {
      val serverScript = java.io.File("/app/applet/server.cjs")
      if (serverScript.exists()) {
        try {
          ProcessBuilder("sh", "-c", "nohup node /app/applet/server.cjs >/dev/null 2>&1 &")
            .directory(java.io.File("/app/applet"))
            .start()
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
      }
    }
    return true
  }
}

val previewServerReady = providers.of(EnsurePreviewServerRunning::class) {}.get()

rootProject.name = "Netcon"

include(":app")
